### Folio 1 (`Page ID: 11928-1` | `235` Words | `10` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> مقدمة: لئن وصفنا عصرنا هذا بعصر الاستقصاء ما كنا بعيدين عن الصواب. فقد قام الباحثون المعاصرون باستقصاء الكلمات والجذور الواردة في المعاجم العربية الضخمة ذات المجلدات المتعددة كلسان العرب وتاج العروس. وقد ساعدهم على ذلك الحواسيب التي تستطيع أن تنجز في بضع ثوانٍ ما لا يستطيع فريق من الباحثين إنجازه في أيام بل في شهور. لقد صممت الآن برامج حاسوبية لاستقصاء مفردات القرآن الكريم، وموضوعاته؛ وبإمكان القارئ أن يستعرض - بلمسة زرٍّ - جميع المواضع التي وردت فيها كلمة ما، أو جميع الآيات التي تعالج موضوعاً ما.لقد بدأ عصر الاستقصاء في مجال القرآن الكريم عام ١٩٣٩م عند ما قدّم محمد فؤاد عبد الباقي ﵀ كتابه الشهير» المعجم المفهرس لألفاظ القرآن الكريم «إلى دار الكتب المصرية بالقاهرة لتقوم بطبعه ونشره على نفقتها، وذلك قبل بدء عصر الحاسوب بمدة. ولا شكَّ أن هذا الكتاب المفيد ساعد - ولا يزال يساعد - الباحثين والقرَّاء في استقصاء المسائل اللغوية، غير أنه لا يسعف من أراد استقصاء مسألة من المسائل النحوية، نحو الآيات التي وردت فيها» إن «الشرطية، أو» لَمَّا «الحينية، أو الآيات التي تحتوي على ضمير من الضمائر - مثلاً -، إذ لم يذكر فيه المؤلف حروف المعاني ولا الضمائر.وقد سدَّ هذا الفراغ المؤلفان الدكتور إسماعيل أحمد عمايرة، والدكتور عبد الحميد مصطفى السيد بكتابهما القيم» معجم الأدوات والضمائر في القرآن الكريم «الذي أصدراه عام ١٤٠٧هـ/ ١٩٨٦م.وفي عام ١٤١٧هـ/ ١٩٩٦م صدر كتاب آخر في الموضوع نفسه وهو» معجم حروف المعاني في القرآن الكريم «لمؤلفه محمد حسن الشريف.

### Folio 5 (`Page ID: 11928-5` | `199` Words | `10` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> معجم الأدوات والضمائر في القرآن الكريم: هذا المعجم – كما يدلّ عليه اسمه – يفهرس الأدوات والضمائر الواردة في القرآن الكريم. ولا بدّ هنا من توضيح المراد بـ» الأدوات «في هذا المعجم، قال واضعاه:((ونستميح القارئ عذراً عن استخدام كلمة» الأدوات «بدلاً من المصطلح الشائع» حروف المعاني «، فهذه الكلمة أوفى بالحاجة من المصطلح المركب من كلمتين (حروف المعاني) فإن من الحروف ما هو خالص في الحرفية كالباء والفاء وبل، ومنها ما يجمع بين الاسمية والحرفية والفعلية كـ» ما «و» حاشا «و» عدا «، وهو على أيّ حال مصطلح كوفيّ قديم فضلاً عن تجدد استعماله لدى المحدثين. (١) اهـيفهم من كلامهما هذا أن الأدوات هي حروف المعاني نفسها، غير أنهما يفضلان مصطلح الأدوات لكونها أشمل فتندرج تحتها الحروف وغير الحروف. وهذه التسمية لا غبار عليها، فقد استعملها السيوطي في الإتقان، وقال: "وأعني بالأدوات الحروف وما شاكلها من الأسماء والأفعال والظروف" (٢) . وقد ذكر الذين ألَّفوا في حروف المعاني أسماءً مثل: أيّ، وغير، وكلّ، ومَن؛ وظروفاً مثل: إذا، وثمَّ، ومتى، ومع؛ وأفعالاً مثل خلا، وعدا، وعسى، وليس.وكان المفروض أن يحدد المؤلفان مرادهما بالأدوات، إذ لم يرد تعريف لها عند العلماء، ولا تحديد لعددها، فبينما ذكر الهرويّ واحداً وأربعين حرفاً
(١) ص: م ١٠.(٢) الإتقان ٢: ١٤٠.

### Folio 6 (`Page ID: 11928-6` | `191` Words | `8` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> في الأزهية، ذكر الزجاجي سبعة وثلاثين ومائة حرف في كتابه حروف المعاني. أما مؤلفا معجم الأدوات والضمائر فقد ذكرا ١٠٨ أداة، منها أسماء الإشارة كهذا، وهذه، وذلك، وتلك، وهؤلاء، وأولئك، وهذان، وذانك؛ والأسماء الموصولة كـ الذي، والذين، واللذان، والتي، واللائي، واللاتي. ولم يذكر أحد ممن ألَّف في هذا المجال» الذي «وفروعه من ضمن حروف المعاني إلاَّ الإربلِّي في ((جواهر الأدب)) حيث ذكره على أساس كونه حرفاً موصولاً في قوله تعالى: ﴿وَخُضْتُمْ كَالَّذِي خَاضُوا﴾ [التوبة ٦٩] عند بعض النحاة (١) .مآخذ على الكتاب:١) لم يسلك المؤلفان مسلكاً موحداً في معالجة الأدوات، فقد ذكرا بعض الأدوات بصورتيها المجرَّدة والمتصلة بالضمائر تحت عنوان واحد كما فعلا بـ» إنَّ «، و» أنَّ «، و» لكنَّ «. وفي أدوات أخرى فَرَّقا بين صورتيها المجردة والمتصلة بالضمائر، بل جعلا صورتها المتَّصلة بكل ضمير أداةً مستقلة كما في» لعلَّ «، و» ليت «، و» مع «. وأدّتْ هذه الطريقة إلى التفريق بين أفراد أسرة واحدة، وإدخال أجنبي بينها، فقد فُرِّقَ بين» أيّكم «و» أيّنا «بـ» أيّما «لأن الترتيب الأبجدي يقتضي ذلك. وكان الأَوْلى أن تذكر الأداة المجردة تحت عنوان رئيس، وتذكر صورها المتصلة بالضمائر تحتها على النحو التالي:لعلَّ* لعل*لعله

> * لعلهم* لعلك* لعلكم٢) ومن مظاهر تعدد المنهج كذلك: أنهما جعلا نون التوكيد الخفيفة مع الثقيلة في حين أنهما فرَّقا بين» كلَّما «المتصلة و» كلَّ ما «المنفصلة، مع أن الفرق بين النونين فرق في اللفظ، أما في» كلَّما «فهو فرق في الإملاء، وكان الأولى أن يقال إنه في آيتين كتبت» ما «منفصلة في هذه الكلمة.٣) منهج الواضعين في ذكر» الأدوات «هو مراعاة شكلها دون دلالتها، فضمَّا» لما «الحينيَّة إلى» لما «الجازمة، و» لما «الاستثنائية؛ و» إما «العاطفة إلى» إما «الشرطية المكونة من» إنْ «و» ما «الزائدة؛ ولم يفرقا بين أنواع اللام، فذكرا الجارة، والمزحلقة، والفارقة، ولام الابتداء، ولام الأمر، ولام تلقي القسم كلها تحت عنوان اللام. وقالا موضحين منهجهما في ذلك:» إن هذا العمل فهرسة فحسب، وهو يقوم في أساسه على مراعاة الشكل، ولو فعلنا غير ذلك لوجدنا أننا نبتعد عن الهدف، فنحن نرمي بهذه الفهرسة إلى أن نيسِّر السبيل على الدارس، فنضع بين يديه الأداة الواحدة في جميع استعمالاتها القرآنية ضمن سياقها النصيّ (١) «اهـ. وإذا كان المراد بهذا عدم مراعاة الفروق بين دلالات الأداة الواحدة كالظرفية، والمصاحبة، والتعليل في الحرف» في «فهو قول وجيه؛ أما أن تعد» إنْ «الشرطية، و» إنْ «النافية، و» إنْ «المخففة من الثقيلة أداة واحدة لاتفاقها في الشكل فهذا كلام مجانب للصواب. ثم أي تيسير للباحث الذي يزمع دراسة لام الأمر – مثلاً – في أن يجد شواهدها القرآنية مبثوثة في خضمّ من الشواهد للامات مختلفة؟

### Folio 8 (`Page ID: 11928-8` | `167` Words | `11` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> معجم حروف المعاني في القرآن الكريم: أورد فيه المؤلف ١١٢حرفاً من حروف المعاني الواردة في القرآن الكريم. وهذه الحروف تنقسم خمسة أقسام:١) حروف المعاني مثل: في، وعلى، ونعم، وهمزة الاستفهام، والسين؛ وعددها نحو ستين.٢) أسماء نحو: أين، وكيف، وغير، وما الموصولة، وما الاستفهامية؛ وعددها نحو ١٧.٣) حروف البناء الصرفي مثل ألف التأنيث المقصورة، وألف التأنيث الممدودة، وألف جمع المؤنث.٤) حروف الدلالة النحوية مثل: الألف والواو والياء الدالّة على الحالات الإعرابية في الأسماء الخمسة، والمثنى، وجمع المذكر السالم.٥) ألف التثنية كما في» يفعلان «وهي الفاعل.بلغ عدد المداخل في الأقسام الثلاثة الأخيرة نحو ثلاثين مدخلاً.منهج المؤلف في الكتاب:قبل إيراد شواهد الحرف من القرآن الكريم، يذكر تعريفاً موجزاً له، ويذكر أنواعه إذا كانت له أنواع، وإذا كانت له أكثر من دلالة، يذكرها، ويضع لكل معنى رمزاً إما بالحرف وإما بالرسم، ويثبت هذه الرمز الدالّ على معناه مع الشاهد القرآني. ذكر لهمزة الاستفهام – مثلاً – اثنين وعشرين معنىً، واختار لكل معنى رمزاً من الرموز المتاحة في الحاسوب كما يتضح من الأمثلة الآتية:

### Folio 12 (`Page ID: 11928-12` | `210` Words | `11` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> دراسات لأساليب القرآن الكريم: هو كتاب ضخم يقع في أحد عشر مجلداً، وينقسم إلى ثلاثة أقسام؛ ويحتوي القسم الأول على ثلاثة أجزاء، ويحتوي كل من القسمين الثاني والثالث على أربعة أجزاء. خصص القسم الأول لدراسة حروف المعاني، والقسم الثاني لدراسة مباحث الصرف، والقسم الثالث لدراسة مباحث النحو.عكف المؤلف على إنجازه خمسة وعشرين عاماً، وهو كتاب كبير في مبناه، وعظيم في معناه. أما كبر مبناه فيظهر من قول المؤلف: إنه» الآيات والقراءات في هذا المبحث، أو أشير إليها (٢٨٧٠٠) «اهـ؛ وأما عظم معناه فيتضح من قول الشيخ محمود محمد شاكر ﵀ في تصديره للكتاب إن المؤلف أودعه» معرفة واسعة مستوعبة تامة لدقائق علم النحو، وعلم الصرف، وعلم اختلاف الأساليب «.يرى المؤلف أن هذا الكتاب معجم نحوي صرفي للقرآن الكريم، ويقول موضحاً هدفه من وضعه:» استهدفت أن أضع للقرآن الكريم معجماً نحوياً صرفياً يكون مرجعاً لدارس النحو، فيستطيع أن يعرف متى أراد: أوقع مثل هذا الأسلوب في القرآن أم لا؟ وإذا كان في القرآن فهل ورد كثيراً أو قليلاً، وفي قراءات متواترة أو شاذّة؟ كما أنه يستطيع أن يحتكم إليه في الموازنة بين الأقوال المختلفة كما كان يفعل الصدر الأول في الاحتكام إلى كلام الفصحاء ومشافهتهم قبل أن يدبَّ اللحن إلى الألسنة « (١) .
(١) دراسات لأسلوب القرآن الكريم ١: ١: ١. (القسم الأول: الجزء الأول: ص ١) .

### Folio 13 (`Page ID: 11928-13` | `199` Words | `9` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> غير أنه ليس معجماً بالمعنى المعروف والشكل المألوف، إنما هو كتاب يدرس فيه المؤلف المسائل الصرفية والنحوية الواردة في القرآن الكريم، والآيات المجموعة الواردة في الكتاب إنما جمعها المؤلف خدمة لهذه الدراسة. ولكن الكتاب يحوي نواة للمعجم، ويمكن تحويل المواد الواردة فيه إلى معجم.منهج الكتاب:١) يبدأ المؤلف دراسة مسألة ما تحت عنوان» لمحات عن دراسة (١) في القرآن الكريم «يذكر فيه ما يمهد لدراسة المسألة دراسة مفصلة في المبحث الذي يليه. يقول المؤلف موضحاً هدفه من هذا العنوان:» رأيت أن أقدم أمام دراسة كل حرف (٢) صورة واضحة موجزة لعناصر الدراسة التفصيلية، واخترت لها عنوان (لمحات عن دراسة) ، وهذه اللمحات أشبه بما تفعله الإذاعات في صدر نشراتها الإخبارية من تقديم موجز الأنباء، وآثرت هذا المنهج لأمرين:أ) تقريب هذه الدراسة إلى نفوس القرَّاء على اختلاف درجاتهم الثقافية، وتيسيرها لهم، فمن شاء اكتفى بهذا القدر، ومن شاء رجع إلى الدراسة التفصيلية.ب) كفل هذا المنهج لي حريَّة نقل النصوص في الدراسة التفصيلية «.٢) ثم يعقد فصلاً بعنوان» دراسة في القرآن الكريم «يفصل فيه الكلام على وجوهها مع ذكر شواهد كثيرة من القرآن الكريم.
(١) هنا يذكر المسألة المدروسة.(٢) إنما ذكر الحرف لأنه بدأ مشروعه هذا بدراسة حروف المعاني، ثم أكمله بدراسة المسائل الصرفية والنحوية كلها.

### Folio 14 (`Page ID: 11928-14` | `212` Words | `11` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> لقد وجد الشيخ عضيمة باستقراء بعض المسائل النحوية في القرآن الكريم أن ما جاء في القرآن الكريم يخالف بعض القواعد النحوية التي ذكرها النحاة. يقول الشيخ موضحاً هذه النتائج التي توصَّل إليها:وللنحويين قوانين كثيرة لم يحتكموا فيها لأسلوب القرآن، فمنعوا أساليب كثيرة جاء نظيرها في القرآن، من ذلك:١) ذكر سيبويه قبح» كلّ «المضافة إلى نكرة في أن يلي العوامل، فقال (١: ٢٧٤) :» " أكلت شاة كلَّ شاةٍ " حسن، و" أكلت كلَّ شاة " ضعيف «.جاء» كلّ «المضافة إلى نكرة مفعولاً به في ٣٦ موضعاً في القرآن الكريم، كما تصرّفت في وجوه كثيرة من الإعراب.٢) منع السهيلي أن تلى» كلّ «المقطوعة عن الإضافة العوامل، نحو:» ضربت كلاً «، و» مررت بكلٍّ « (نتائج الفكر ص ٢٢٧) .جاءت» كلّ «المقطوعة عن الإضافة مفعولاً به، ومجرورة بالحرف متأخرة عن فعلها في آيات من القرآن.٣) اشترط الزمخشري في خبر» أنَّ «الواقعة بعد» لو «أن يكون خبرها فعلاً (المفصَّل ٢: ٢١٦) .جاء خبرها في القرآن اسماً جامداً، واسماً مشتقاً.٤) منع ابن الطراوة أن يقع المصدر المؤوَّل من» أن «والفعل مضافاً إليه (الهمع ٢: ٣) .جاء المصدر المؤوَّل من» أن «والفعل مضافاً إليه في ثلاثة وثلاثين موضعاً في القرآن.٥) منع النحويون وقوع الاستثناء المفرَّغ بعد الإيجاب، وعلَّلوا ذلك بأن وقوعه بعد الإيجاب يتضمن المحال أو الكذب.

### Folio 15 (`Page ID: 11928-15` | `61` Words | `4` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> وفي القرآن ثماني عشرة آية وقع فيها الاستثناء المفرغ بعد الإيجاب، وفي بعضها كان الإيجاب مؤكداً مما يبعد تأويله بالنفي، كقوله تعالى:* ﴿وَإِنَّهَا لَكَبِيرَةٌ إِلَّا عَلَى الْخَاشِعِينَ﴾ (٢: ٤٥) .* ﴿وَإِنْ كَانَتْ لَكَبِيرَةً إِلَّا عَلَى الَّذِينَ هَدَى اللَّهُ﴾ (٢: ١٤٣) .* ﴿لَتَأْتُنَّنِي بِهِ إِلَّا أَنْ يُحَاطَ بِكُمْ﴾ (١٢: ٦٦) .ذكر المؤلف أمثلة أخرى، ونحن نكتفي بهذا القدر.

**English**:
> And in the Qur'an price ten a verse (āyah) happen, come to pass in it exception (al-Istithnāʾ) the finished after affirmation (al-ījāb), And in some of her was affirmation (al-ījāb) emphasize regarding what after its interpretation (taʾwīl) in the negation of, like his word, speech, saying (Exalted is He): * ﴿And seek help through patience and the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh), and indeed, it is difficult except for the humbly submissive to Allāh﴾ (2: 45). * ﴿And thus we have made you a just community that you will be witnesses over the people and the Messenger will be a witness over you. And We did not make the qiblah which you used to face except that We might make evident who would follow the Messenger from who would turn back on his heels. And indeed, it is difficult except for those whom Allāh has guided. And never would Allāh have caused you to lose your faith. Indeed Allāh is ever, to the people, Kind and Merciful.﴾ (2: 143). * ﴿Jacob said, "Never will I send him with you until you give me a promise by Allāh that you will bring him back to me, unless you should be surrounded by enemies." And when they had given their promise, he said, "Allāh, over what we say, is Witness."﴾ (12: 66). mentioned (Dhikr) The Author (al-Muʾallif) proverb the other, and we suffice with Divine Decree (al-Qadar).

### Folio 16 (`Page ID: 11928-16` | `161` Words | `6` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> نتائج واقتراحات: يتضح من هذه الدراسة أنه لم يوضع إلى الآن معجم شامل للمسائل الصرفية والنحوية الواردة في القرآن الكريم، ونقترح أن يصمم برنامج حاسوبي لمثل هذا المعجم، ويضمَّن إمكان إعادة تصنيف الآيات الخاصة بمسألة ما تصنيفات فرعية، ولنشرح هذه الفكرة بمثال. ففي مجال المفعول المطلق – مثلاً – تظهر أولاً جميع الآيات التي تتضمن المفعول المطلق. وبإمكاننا توزيع هذه الشواهد على أساس الغرض من المصدر، فتتوزع على الأصناف الآتية:(١) ما جاء فيه المصدر مؤكداً لعامله(٢) ما جاء فيه المصدر مبيناً لنوعه(٣) ما جاء فيه المصدر مبيناً لعدده(٤) ما جاء فيه المصدر نائباً عن فعلهوبلمسة أخرى لأحد المفاتيح نستطيع أن نوزعها على أساس العناصر التي نابت عن المصدر.مما لا شك فيه أن مثل هذا البرنامج سيمنح الباحثين في مجال النحو إمكانات هائلة لدراسة المسائل النحوية والصرفية الواردة في القرآن الكريم، وكذلك سيساعد مؤلفي كتب النحو الدراسية في اختيار الشواهد المناسبة للمسائل النحوية إن شاء الله.ونذيل البحث بنموذجين لتصنيف الأدوات تصنيفاً يساعد الباحث على دراستها.

### Folio 17 (`Page ID: 11928-17` | `138` Words | `12` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> اللام المزحلقة: هي لام الابتداء بعد» إنَّ «المكسورة، وسميت مزحلقة لأنها زحلقت عن صدر الجملة كراهية ابتداء الكلام بمؤكدَينِ. حقها أن تدخل على خبر» إن «، وتدخل كذلك:١) على اسمها إذا تأخر عن الخبر، وكان نكرة.٢) وعلى ضمير الفصل.٣) وعلى ضمير الرفع المنفصل المؤكد للضمير المتَّصل.(أ) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على الخبر المفرد(٢: ٤٥) ﴿وَإِنَّهَا لَكَبِيرَةٌ إِلَّا عَلَى الْخَاشِعِينَ﴾ .(٢: ٧٠) ﴿وَإِنَّا إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ لَمُهْتَدُونَ﴾ .(٢: ١٤٣) ﴿إِنَّ اللَّهَ بِالنَّاسِ لَرَؤُوفٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾(٢: ١٤٩) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَلْحَقُّ مِنْ رَبِّكَ﴾ .(٢: ٢٤٣) ﴿إِنَّ اللَّهَ لَذُو فَضْلٍ عَلَى النَّاسِ﴾ .(٣: ٦٨) ﴿ِنَّ أَوْلَى النَّاسِ بِإِبْرَاهِيمَ لَلَّذِينَ اتَّبَعُو﴾ هُ.(٣: ٩٦) ﴿إِنَّ أَوَّلَ بَيْتٍ وُضِعَ لِلنَّاسِ لَلَّذِي بِبَكَّةَ﴾(٥: ٣٢) ﴿ثُمَّ إِنَّ كَثِيراً مِنْهُمْ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ فِي الْأَرْضِ لَمُسْرِفُونَ﴾ .(٥: ٤٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ كَثِيراً مِنَ النَّاسِ لَفَاسِقُونَ﴾ .(٦: ٢٨) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَكَاذِبُونَ﴾ .

(whether) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon the Predicate (al-Khabar) the singular (al-Mufrad) (2: 45) ﴿And seek help through patience and the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh), and indeed, it is difficult except for the humbly submissive to Allāh﴾. (2: 70) ﴿They said, "Call upon your Sovereign Lord to make clear to us what it is. Indeed, All cows look alike to us. And indeed we, if Allāh wills, will be guided."﴾. (2: 143) ﴿And thus we have made you a just community that you will be witnesses over the people and the Messenger will be a witness over you. And We did not make the qiblah which you used to face except that We might make evident who would follow the Messenger from who would turn back on his heels. And indeed, it is difficult except for those whom Allāh has guided. And never would Allāh have caused you to lose your faith. Indeed Allāh is ever, to the people, Kind and Merciful.﴾ (2: 149) ﴿So from wherever you go out for the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh), O Messenger turn your face toward al- Masjid al-Haram, and indeed, it is the truth from your Sovereign Lord. And Allāh is not unaware of what you do.﴾. (2: 243) ﴿Have you not considered those who left their homes in many thousands, fearing death? Allāh said to them, "Die"; then He restored them to life. And Allāh is ever full of bounty to the people, but most of the people do not show gratitude.﴾. (3: 68) ﴿Indeed, the most worthy of Abraham among the people are those who followed him in submission to Allāh and this prophet, and those who maintain conviction in established truth in his message. And Allāh is ever the ally of those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn).﴾ AH. (3: 96) ﴿Indeed, the first House of worship established for mankind was that at Makkah - blessed and a guidance for the worlds.﴾ (5: 32) ﴿Because of that, We decreed upon the Children of Israel that whoever kills a soul unless for a soul or for corruption done in the land - it is as if he had slain mankind entirely. And whoever saves one - it is as if he had saved mankind entirely. And our messengers had certainly come to them with clear proofs. Then indeed many of them, even after that, throughout the land, were transgressors.﴾. (5: 49) ﴿And judge, O Messenger, between them by what Allāh has revealed and do not follow their inclinations and beware of them, lest they tempt you away from some of what Allāh has revealed to you. And if they turn away - then know that Allāh only intends to afflict them with some of their own sins. And indeed, many among the people are defiantly disobedient.﴾. (6: 28) and indeed them liar.

### Folio 18 (`Page ID: 11928-18` | `153` Words | `20` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٦: ١٢١) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَفِسْقٌ﴾ .(٦: ١٢١) ﴿وَإِنْ أَطَعْتُمُوهُمْ إِنَّكُمْ لَمُشْرِكُونَ﴾ .(٦: ١٣٤) ﴿إِنَّ مَا تُوعَدُونَ لَآتٍ﴾ .(٦: ١٤٦) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَصَادِقُونَ﴾ .(٦: ١٦٥) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(٧: ١٠٩) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَسَاحِرٌ عَلِيمٌ﴾ .(٧: ١٢٣) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَمَكْرٌ﴾(٧: ١٥٣) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ مِنْ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(٧: ١٦٧) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَسَرِيعُ الْعِقَابِ﴾ .(٧: ١٦٧) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(٨: ٥) ﴿وَإِنَّ فَرِيقاً مِنَ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ لَكَارِهُونَ﴾ .(٨: ٤٢) ﴿وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَسَمِيعٌ عَلِيمٌ﴾ .(٩: ٤٢) ﴿وَاللَّهُ يَعْلَمُ إِنَّهُمْ لَكَاذِبُونَ﴾ .(٩: ٤٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ جَهَنَّمَ لَمُحِيطَةٌ بِالْكَافِرِينَ﴾ .(٩: ١١٤) ﴿إِنَّ إِبْرَاهِيمَ لَأَوَّاهٌ حَلِيمٌ﴾ .(١٠: ٥٣) ﴿قُلْ إِي وَرَبِّي إِنَّهُ لَحَقٌّ﴾(١٠: ٦٠) ﴿إِنَّ اللَّهَ لَذُو فَضْلٍ عَلَى النَّاسِ﴾ .(١٠: ٧٦) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَسِحْرٌ مُبِينٌ﴾ .(١٠: ٨٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ فِرْعَوْنَ لَعَالٍ فِي الْأَرْضِ﴾ .(١٠: ٩٢) ﴿وَإِنَّ كَثِيراً مِنَ النَّاسِ عَنْ آيَاتِنَا لَغَافِلُونَ﴾ .(١١: ٩) ﴿إِنَّهُ لَيَؤُوسٌ كَفُورٌ﴾ .(١١: ١٠) ﴿إِنَّهُ لَفَرِحٌ فَخُورٌ﴾ .

**English**:
> (6: 121) and that he disobedience. (6: 121) ﴿And do not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has not been mentioned, for indeed, it is grave disobedience. And indeed do the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) inspire their allies among men to dispute with you. And if you were to obey them, indeed, you would be associators of others with Him.﴾. (6: 134) ﴿Indeed, what you are promised is coming, and you will not cause failure to Allāh.﴾. (6: 146) indeed, truly truthful. (6: 165) ﴿And it is He who has made you successors upon the terrestrial earth and has raised some of you above others in degrees of rank that He may try you through what He has given you. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is swift in penalty; but indeed, He is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (7: 109) ﴿Said the eminent among the people of Pharaoh, "Indeed, this is a learned magician﴾. (7: 123) ﴿Said Pharaoh, "You believed in him before I gave you permission. Indeed, this is a conspiracy which you conspired in the city to expel therefrom its people. But you are going to know.﴾ (7: 153) ﴿But those who committed misdeeds and then repented after them and believed - indeed your Sovereign Lord, thereafter, is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (7: 167) ﴿And mention when your Sovereign Lord declared that He would surely continue to send upon them until the Day of Resurrection and Accounting (Yawm al-Qiyāmah) those who would afflict them with the worst torment. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is swift in penalty; but indeed, He is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (7: 167) ﴿And it is He who has made you successors upon the terrestrial earth and has raised some of you above others in degrees of rank that He may try you through what He has given you. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is swift in penalty; but indeed, He is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (8: 5) ﴿It is just as when your Sovereign Lord brought you out of your home for the battle of Badr in truth, while indeed, a party among those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn) were unwilling,﴾. (8: 42) ﴿Remember when you were on the near side of the valley, and they were on the farther side, and the caravan was lower in position than you. If you had made an appointment to meet, you would have missed the appointment. But it was so that Allāh might accomplish a matter already destined - that those who perished through disbelief would perish upon evidence and those who lived in faith would live upon evidence; and indeed, Allāh is ever Hearing and Knowing.﴾. (9: 42) ﴿Had it been an easy gain and a moderate trip, the subversive double-faced deceivers (Al-Munāfiqūn) would have followed you, but distant to them was the journey. And they will swear by Allāh, "If we were able, we would have gone forth with you," destroying themselves through false oaths, and Allāh knows that indeed they are liars.﴾. (9: 49) ﴿And among them is he who says, "Permit me to remain at home and do not put me to trial." Unquestionably, into trial they have fallen. And indeed, the Hellfire of retributive consequence (Jahannam) will encompass the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn).﴾. (9: 114) ﴿And the request of forgiveness of Abraham for his father was only because of a promise he had made to him. But when it became apparent to Abraham that his father was an enemy to Allāh, he disassociated himself from him. Indeed was Abraham compassionate and patient.﴾. (10: 53) ﴿And they ask information of you, O Messenger, "Is it true?" Say, "Yes, by my Sovereign Lord. Indeed, it is truth; and you will not cause failure to Allāh."﴾ (10: 60) ﴿Have you not considered those who left their homes in many thousands, fearing death? Allāh said to them, "Die"; then He restored them to life. And Allāh is ever full of bounty to the people, but most of the people do not show gratitude.﴾. (10: 76) ﴿Have the people been amazed that We revealed revelation to a man from among them, saying, "Warn mankind and give good tidings to those who maintain conviction in established truth that they will have a firm precedence of honor with their Sovereign Lord"? But the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn) say, "Indeed, this is an obvious magician."﴾. (10: 83) ﴿But no one believed Moses, except some youths among his people, for fear of Pharaoh and his establishment that they would persecute them. And indeed, Pharaoh was haughty within the land, and indeed, he was of the transgressors﴾. (10: 92) ﴿So today We will save you in body that you may be to those who succeed you a sign. And indeed, many among the people, of Our signs, are heedless﴾. (11: 9) indeed He Līʾūs very ungrateful. (11: 10) ﴿But if We give him a taste of favor after hardship has touched him, he will surely say, "Bad times have left me." Indeed, he is exultant and boastful -﴾.

### Folio 19 (`Page ID: 11928-19` | `139` Words | `21` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (١١: ٤١) ﴿إِنَّ رَبِّي لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١١: ٧٢) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَشَيْءٌ عَجِيبٌ﴾ .(١١: ٧٥) ﴿إِنَّ إِبْرَاهِيمَ لَحَلِيمٌ أَوَّاهٌ مُنِيبٌ﴾ .(١٢: ١١) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَهُ لَنَاصِحُونَ﴾ .(١٢: ١٢) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَهُ لَحَافِظُونَ﴾ .(١٢: ١٤) ﴿إِنَّا إِذاً لَخَاسِرُونَ﴾ .(١٢: ٥٣) ﴿إِنَّ النَّفْسَ لَأَمَّارَةٌ بِالسُّوءِ﴾ .(١٢: ٦١) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَفَاعِلُونَ﴾ .(١٢: ٦٨) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَذُو عِلْمٍ﴾ .(١٢: ٧٠) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَسَارِقُونَ﴾ .(١٢: ٧٩) ﴿إِنَّا إِذاً لَظَالِمُونَ﴾ .١٢: ٨٢) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَصَادِقُونَ﴾ .(١٣: ٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَذُو مَغْفِرَةٍ﴾ .(١٣: ٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَشَدِيدُ الْعِقَابِ﴾ .(١٤: ٧) ﴿إِنَّ عَذَابِي لَشَدِيدٌ﴾ .(١٤: ٨) ﴿فَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَغَنِيٌّ حَمِيدٌ﴾ .(١٤: ٣٤) ﴿إِنَّ الْأِنْسَانَ لَظَلُومٌ كَفَّارٌ﴾(١٤: ٣٩) ﴿إِنَّ رَبِّي لَسَمِيعُ الدُّعَاءِ﴾ .(١٥: ٦) ﴿إِنَّكَ لَمَجْنُونٌ﴾ .(١٥: ٤٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ جَهَنَّمَ لَمَوْعِدُهُمْ أَجْمَعِينَ﴾ .(١٥: ٥٩) ﴿إِنَّا لَمُنَجُّوهُمْ أَجْمَعِينَ﴾ .(١٥: ٦٤) ﴿وَأَتَيْنَاكَ بِالْحَقِّ وَإِنَّا لَصَادِقُونَ﴾ .

**English**:
> (11: 41) ﴿And Noah said, "Embark therein; in the name of Allāh is ever its course and its anchorage. Indeed, my Sovereign Lord is Forgiving and Merciful."﴾. (11: 72) ﴿She said, "Woe to me! Shall I give birth while I am an old woman and this, my husband, is an old man? Indeed, this is an amazing thing!"﴾. (11: 75) ﴿Indeed, Abraham was forbearing, grieving and frequently returning to Allāh.﴾. (12: 11) ﴿They said, "O our father, why do you not entrust us with Joseph while indeed, we are to him sincere counselors?﴾. (12: 12) ﴿Send him with us tomorrow that he may eat well and play. And indeed, we will be his guardians.﴾. (12: 14) ﴿They said, "If a wolf should eat him while we are a strong clan, indeed, we would then be losers."﴾. (12: 53) ﴿And I do not acquit myself. Indeed, the soul is a persistent enjoiner of evil, except those upon which my Sovereign Lord has mercy. Indeed, my Sovereign Lord is Forgiving and Merciful."﴾. (12: 61) indeed, truly observant. (12: 68) ﴿And when they entered from where their father had ordered them, it did not avail them against Allāh at all except it was a need within the soul of Jacob, which he satisfied. And indeed, he was a possessor of knowledge because of what We had taught him, but most of the people do not know.﴾. (12: 70) how thieves. (12: 79) ﴿He said, "I seek the refuge of Allāh to prevent that we take except him with whom we found our possession. Indeed, we would then be unjust."﴾. 12: 82) indeed, truly truthful. (13: 6) ﴿They impatiently urge you to bring about evil before good, while there has already occurred before them similar punishments to what they demand. And indeed, your Sovereign Lord is full of forgiveness for the people despite their wrongdoing, and indeed, your Sovereign Lord is severe in penalty.﴾. (13: 6) ﴿They impatiently urge you to bring about evil before good, while there has already occurred before them similar punishments to what they demand. And indeed, your Sovereign Lord is full of forgiveness for the people despite their wrongdoing, and indeed, your Sovereign Lord is severe in penalty.﴾. (14: 7) ﴿And remember when your Sovereign Lord proclaimed, 'If you are grateful, I will surely increase you in favor; but if you deny, indeed, My punishment is severe.' "﴾. (14: 8) ﴿And Moses said, "If you should disbelieve, you and whoever is on the terrestrial earth entirely - indeed, Allāh is ever Free of need and Praiseworthy."﴾. (14: 34) ﴿And He gave you from all you asked of Him. And if you should count the favor of Allāh, you could not enumerate them. Indeed, mankind is generally most unjust and ungrateful.﴾ (14: 39) ﴿Praise to Allāh, who has granted to me in old age Ishmael and Isaac. Indeed, my Sovereign Lord is the Hearer of supplication.﴾. (15: 6) how mad. (15: 43) ﴿And indeed, the Hellfire of retributive consequence (Jahannam) is the promised place for them all.﴾. (15: 59) ﴿Except the family of Lot; indeed, we will save them all﴾. (15: 64) ﴿And we have come to you with truth, and indeed, we are truthful.﴾.

### Folio 20 (`Page ID: 11928-20` | `154` Words | `17` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (١٥: ٨٥) ﴿وَإِنَّ السَّاعَةَ لَآتِيَةٌ﴾ .(١٦: ٧) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكُمْ لَرَؤُوفٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١٦: ١٨) ﴿إِنَّ اللَّهَ لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١٦: ٤٧) ﴿فَإِنَّ رَبَّكُمْ لَرَؤُوفٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١٦: ٨٦) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَكَاذِبُونَ﴾ .(١٦: ١١٠) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ مِنْ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١٦: ١١٩) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ مِنْ بَعْدِهَا لَغَفُورٌ رَحِيمٌ﴾ .(١٧: ٤٩) ﴿أَإِنَّا لَمَبْعُوثُونَ خَلْقاً جَدِيداً﴾ .(١٧: ٩٨) ﴿أَإِنَّا لَمَبْعُوثُونَ خَلْقاً جَدِيداً﴾ .(١٨: ٨) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَجَاعِلُونَ مَا عَلَيْهَا صَعِيداً جُرُزاً﴾ .(ب) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على الخبر الجملة:(٢: ١٤٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ الَّذِينَ أُوتُوا الْكِتَابَ لَيَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّهُ الْحَقُّ﴾(٢: ١٤٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ فَرِيقاً مِنْهُمْ لَيَكْتُمُونَ الْحَقَّ﴾ .(٦: ١٩) ﴿أَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَشْهَدُونَ أَنَّ مَعَ اللَّهِ آلِهَةً أُخْرَى﴾ .(٦: ٣٣) ﴿قَدْ نَعْلَمُ إِنَّهُ لَيَحْزُنُكَ الَّذِي يَقُولُونَ﴾ .(٦: ١١٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ كَثِيراً لَيُضِلُّونَ بِأَهْوَائِهِمْ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ﴾(٦: ١٢١) ﴿وَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَى أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ﴾ .(٧: ٦٠) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَرَاكَ فِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(٧: ٦٦) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَرَاكَ فِي سَفَاهَةٍ﴾ .(٧: ٦٦) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَنَظُنُّكَ مِنَ الْكَاذِبِينَ﴾ .

**English**:
> (15: 85) ﴿And We have not created the heavens and the terrestrial earth and that between them except in truth. And indeed, the Hour is coming; so forgive with gracious forgiveness.﴾. (16: 7) ﴿And they carry your loads to a land you could not have reached except with difficulty to yourselves. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is Kind and Merciful.﴾. (16: 18) ﴿And if you should count the favors of Allāh, you could not enumerate them. Indeed, Allāh is ever Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (16: 47) ﴿Or that He would not seize them gradually in a state of dread? But indeed, your Sovereign Lord is Kind and Merciful.﴾. (16: 86) how liar. (16: 110) ﴿But those who committed misdeeds and then repented after them and believed - indeed your Sovereign Lord, thereafter, is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (16: 119) ﴿But those who committed misdeeds and then repented after them and believed - indeed your Sovereign Lord, thereafter, is Forgiving and Merciful.﴾. (17: 49) ﴿And they say, "When we are bones and crumbled particles, will we truly be resurrected as a new creation?"﴾. (17: 98) ﴿And they say, "When we are bones and crumbled particles, will we truly be resurrected as a new creation?"﴾. (18: 8) ﴿And indeed, We will make that which is upon it into a barren ground.﴾. (B) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon the Predicate (al-Khabar) the sentence (al-jumlah): (2: 144) ﴿We have certainly seen the turning of your face, O Messenger, toward the heaven, and We will surely turn you to a qiblah with which you will be pleased. So turn your face toward al-Masjid al-Haram. And wherever you believers are, turn your faces toward it in the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh). Indeed, those who have been given the Inscribed Scripture well know that it is the truth from their Sovereign Lord. And Allāh is not unaware of what they do.﴾ (2: 146) ﴿Those to whom We gave the Inscribed Scripture know him as they know their own sons. But indeed, a party of them conceal the truth while they know it.﴾. (6: 19) ﴿Say, "What thing is greatest in testimony?" Say, "Allāh is ever witness between me and you. And this Qur'an was revealed to me that I may warn you thereby and whomever it reaches. Do you truly testify that with Allāh there are other deities?" Say, "I will not testify with you." Say, "Indeed, He is but one Allāh, and indeed, I am free of what you associate with Him."﴾. (6: 33) ﴿We know that you, O Messenger, are saddened by what they say. And indeed, they do not call you untruthful, but it is the verses of Allāh that the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) reject.﴾. (6: 119) ﴿And why should you not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has been mentioned while He has explained in detail to you what He has forbidden you, excepting that to which you are compelled. And indeed do many lead others astray through their own inclinations without knowledge. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord - He is most knowing of the transgressors.﴾ (6: 121) ﴿And do not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has not been mentioned, for indeed, it is grave disobedience. And indeed do the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) inspire their allies among men to dispute with you. And if you were to obey them, indeed, you would be associators of others with Him.﴾. (7: 60) Indeed, we your Lanr in error, going astray clear, manifest, making clear. (7: 66) Indeed, we your Lanr in foolishness. (7: 66) ﴿Said the eminent ones who disbelieved among his people, "Indeed, we see you in foolishness, and indeed, we think you are of the liars."﴾.

- **[S10] AR**: (١٨: ٨) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَجَاعِلُونَ مَا عَلَيْهَا صَعِيداً جُرُزاً﴾ .
  **[S10] EN**: (18: 8) ﴿And indeed, We will make that which is upon it into a barren ground.﴾.
- **[S11] AR**: (ب) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على الخبر الجملة: (٢: ١٤٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ الَّذِينَ أُوتُوا الْكِتَابَ لَيَعْلَمُونَ أَنَّهُ الْحَقُّ﴾ (٢: ١٤٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ فَرِيقاً مِنْهُمْ لَيَكْتُمُونَ الْحَقَّ﴾ .
  **[S11] EN**: (B) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon the Predicate (al-Khabar) the sentence (al-jumlah): (2: 144) ﴿We have certainly seen the turning of your face, O Messenger, toward the heaven, and We will surely turn you to a qiblah with which you will be pleased. So turn your face toward al-Masjid al-Haram. And wherever you believers are, turn your faces toward it in the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh). Indeed, those who have been given the Inscribed Scripture well know that it is the truth from their Sovereign Lord. And Allāh is not unaware of what they do.﴾ (2: 146) ﴿Those to whom We gave the Inscribed Scripture know him as they know their own sons. But indeed, a party of them conceal the truth while they know it.﴾.
- **[S12] AR**: (٦: ١٩) ﴿أَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَشْهَدُونَ أَنَّ مَعَ اللَّهِ آلِهَةً أُخْرَى﴾ .
  **[S12] EN**: (6: 19) ﴿Say, "What thing is greatest in testimony?" Say, "Allāh is ever witness between me and you. And this Qur'an was revealed to me that I may warn you thereby and whomever it reaches. Do you truly testify that with Allāh there are other deities?" Say, "I will not testify with you." Say, "Indeed, He is but one Allāh, and indeed, I am free of what you associate with Him."﴾.
- **[S13] AR**: (٦: ٣٣) ﴿قَدْ نَعْلَمُ إِنَّهُ لَيَحْزُنُكَ الَّذِي يَقُولُونَ﴾ .
  **[S13] EN**: (6: 33) ﴿We know that you, O Messenger, are saddened by what they say. And indeed, they do not call you untruthful, but it is the verses of Allāh that the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) reject.﴾.
- **[S14] AR**: (٦: ١١٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ كَثِيراً لَيُضِلُّونَ بِأَهْوَائِهِمْ بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ﴾ (٦: ١٢١) ﴿وَإِنَّ الشَّيَاطِينَ لَيُوحُونَ إِلَى أَوْلِيَائِهِمْ﴾ .
  **[S14] EN**: (6: 119) ﴿And why should you not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has been mentioned while He has explained in detail to you what He has forbidden you, excepting that to which you are compelled. And indeed do many lead others astray through their own inclinations without knowledge. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord - He is most knowing of the transgressors.﴾ (6: 121) ﴿And do not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has not been mentioned, for indeed, it is grave disobedience. And indeed do the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) inspire their allies among men to dispute with you. And if you were to obey them, indeed, you would be associators of others with Him.﴾.
- **[S15] AR**: (٧: ٦٠) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَرَاكَ فِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .
  **[S15] EN**: (7: 60) Indeed, we your Lanr in error, going astray clear, manifest, making clear.
- **[S16] AR**: (٧: ٦٦) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَرَاكَ فِي سَفَاهَةٍ﴾ .
  **[S16] EN**: (7: 66) Indeed, we your Lanr in foolishness.
- **[S17] AR**: (٧: ٦٦) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَنَظُنُّكَ مِنَ الْكَاذِبِينَ﴾ .
  **[S17] EN**: (7: 66) ﴿Said the eminent ones who disbelieved among his people, "Indeed, we see you in foolishness, and indeed, we think you are of the liars."﴾.

### Folio 21 (`Page ID: 11928-21` | `186` Words | `18` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٧: ٨١) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ الرِّجَالَ شَهْوَةً مِنْ دُونِ النِّسَاءِ﴾(٩: ٣٤) ﴿إِنَّ كَثِيراً مِنَ الْأَحْبَارِ وَالرُّهْبَانِ لَيَأْكُلُونَ أَمْوَالَ النَّاسِ بِالْبَاطِلِ﴾ .(١١: ٧٩) ﴿وَإِنَّكَ لَتَعْلَمُ مَا نُرِيدُ﴾ .(١١: ٩١) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَنَرَاكَ فِينَا ضَعِيفاً﴾ .(١٢: ٣٠) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَرَاهَا فِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(١٢: ٩٤) ﴿إِنِّي لَأَجِدُ رِيحَ يُوسُفَ﴾ .(١٦: ١٢٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَيَحْكُمُ بَيْنَهُمْ يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ﴾ .(١٧: ٤٠) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَتَقُولُونَ قَوْلاً عَظِيماً﴾ .(١٧: ١٠١) ﴿إِنِّي لَأَظُنُّكَ يَا مُوسَى مَسْحُوراً﴾ .(١٧: ١٠٢) ﴿وَإِنِّي لَأَظُنُّكَ يَا فِرْعَوْنُ مَثْبُوراً﴾ .(٢٣: ٧٣) ﴿وَإِنَّكَ لَتَدْعُوهُمْ إِلَى صِرَاطٍ مُسْتَقِيمٍ﴾ .(٢٥: ٢٠) ﴿إِلَّا إِنَّهُمْ لَيَأْكُلُونَ الطَّعَامَ وَيَمْشُونَ فِي الْأَسْوَاقِ﴾ .(٢٧: ٦) ﴿وَإِنَّكَ لَتُلَقَّى الْقُرْآنَ مِنْ لَدُنْ حَكِيمٍ عَلِيمٍ﴾ .(٢٧: ٥٥) ﴿أَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ الرِّجَالَ شَهْوَةً مِنْ دُونِ النِّسَاءِ﴾ .(٢٧: ٧٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَيَعْلَمُ مَا تُكِنُّ صُدُورُهُمْ وَمَا يُعْلِنُونَ﴾(٢٨: ٣٨) ﴿وَإِنِّي لَأَظُنُّهُ مِنَ الْكَاذِبِينَ﴾ .(٢٨: ٧٦) ﴿وَآتَيْنَاهُ مِنَ الْكُنُوزِ مَا إِنَّ مَفَاتِحَهُ لَتَنُوءُ بِالْعُصْبَةِ أُولِي الْقُوَّةِ﴾ .(٢٩: ٢٨) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ الْفَاحِشَةَ﴾ .(٢٩: ٢٩) ﴿أَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَأْتُونَ الرِّجَالَ﴾(٣٧: ١٣٧) ﴿وَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَمُرُّونَ عَلَيْهِمْ مُصْبِحِينَ﴾ .(٣٧: ١٥١) ﴿أَلا إِنَّهُمْ مِنْ إِفْكِهِمْ لَيَقُولُونَ﴾(٣٨: ٢٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ كَثِيراً مِنَ الْخُلَطَاءِ لَيَبْغِي بَعْضُهُمْ عَلَى بَعْضٍ﴾ .

**English**:
> (7: 81) ﴿Indeed, you approach men with desire, instead of women. Rather, you are a transgressing people."﴾ (9: 34) ﴿O you who have believed, indeed many of the scholars and the monks devour the wealth of people unjustly and avert them from the way of Allāh. And those who hoard gold and silver and spend it not in the way of Allāh - give them tidings of an painful punishment.﴾. (11: 79) ﴿They said, "You have already known that we have not concerning your daughters any claim, and indeed, you know what we want."﴾. (11: 91) indeed, truly your Lanr in us weak. (12: 30) Indeed, we its Lanr in error, going astray clear, manifest, making clear. (12: 94) ﴿And when the caravan departed from Egypt, their father said, "Indeed, I find the smell of Joseph and would say that he was alive if you did not think me weakened in mind."﴾. (16: 124) ﴿The sabbath was only appointed for those who differed over it. And indeed, your Sovereign Lord will judge between them on the Day of Resurrection and Accounting (Yawm al-Qiyāmah) concerning that over which they used to differ.﴾. (17: 40) ﴿Then, has your Sovereign Lord chosen you for having sons and taken from among the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) daughters? Indeed, you say a grave saying.﴾. (17: 101) Indeed, I for your think, suppose, assume O Mūsā magic. (17: 102) indeed, truly for your think, suppose, assume O Pharaoh cry. (23: 73) ﴿And indeed, you invite them to a Straight Path.﴾. (25: 20) ﴿And We did not send before you, O Messenger, any of the messengers except that they ate food and walked in the markets. And We have made some of you people as trial for others - will you have patience? And ever is your Sovereign Lord, Seeing.﴾. (27: 6) and indeed you granted the Qur'an from from, from the presence of All-Wise, wise All-Knowing. (27: 55) ﴿Indeed, you approach men with desire, instead of women. Rather, you are a transgressing people."﴾. (27: 74) ﴿And indeed, your Sovereign Lord knows what their breasts conceal and what they declare.﴾ (28: 38) ﴿And Pharaoh said, "O eminent ones, I have not known you to have a Allāh other than me. Then ignite for me, O Haman, a fire upon the clay and make for me a tower that I may look at the Allāh of Moses. And indeed, I do think he is among the liars."﴾. (28: 76) ﴿Indeed, Qarun was from the people of Moses, but he tyrannized them. And We gave him of treasures whose keys would burden a band of strong men; thereupon his people said to him, "Do not exult. Indeed, Allāh does not like the exultant.﴾. (29: 28) ﴿And mention Lot, when he said to his people, "Indeed, you commit such immorality as no one has preceded you with from among the worlds.﴾. (29: 29) your Aʾn come men (al-Rijāl) (37: 137) ﴿And indeed, you pass by them in the morning﴾. (37: 151) ﴿Unquestionably, it is out of their invented falsehood that they say,﴾ (38: 24) ﴿David said, "He has certainly wronged you in demanding your ewe in addition to his ewes. And indeed, many associates oppress one another, except for those who maintain conviction in established truth and do righteous deeds - and few are they." And David became certain that We had tried him, and he asked forgiveness of his Sovereign Lord and fell down bowing in prostration and turned in repentance to Allāh.﴾.

### Folio 22 (`Page ID: 11928-22` | `149` Words | `14` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٤٠: ٣٧) ﴿وَإِنِّي لَأَظُنُّهُ كَاذِباً﴾(٤٠: ٥١) ﴿إِنَّا لَنَنْصُرُ رُسُلَنَا وَالَّذِينَ آمَنُوا﴾(٤١: ٩) ﴿قُلْ أَإِنَّكُمْ لَتَكْفُرُونَ بِالَّذِي خَلَقَ الْأَرْضَ فِي يَوْمَيْنِ﴾(٤٢: ٥٢) ﴿وَإِنَّكَ لَتَهْدِي إِلَى صِرَاطٍ مُسْتَقِيمٍ﴾ .(٤٣: ٣٧) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَيَصُدُّونَهُمْ عَنِ السَّبِيلِ﴾ .(٥٣: ٢٧) ﴿إِنَّ الَّذِينَ لا يُؤْمِنُونَ بِالْآخِرَةِ لَيُسَمُّونَ الْمَلائِكَةَ تَسْمِيَةَ الْأُنْثَى﴾ .(٥٨: ٢) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَيَقُولُونَ مُنْكَراً مِنَ الْقَوْلِ وَزُوراً﴾ .(٦٩: ٤٩) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَنَعْلَمُ أَنَّ مِنْكُمْ مُكَذِّبِينَ﴾ .(٩٦: ٦) ﴿كَلَّا إِنَّ الْأِنْسَانَ لَيَطْغَى﴾ .(ج) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على الخبر شبه الجملة:(٢: ١٣٠) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ فِي الْآخِرَةِ لَمِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ﴾ .(٢: ١٤٥) ﴿إِنَّكَ إِذاً لَمِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ﴾ .(٢: ١٧٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ الَّذِينَ اخْتَلَفُوا فِي الْكِتَابِ لَفِي شِقَاقٍ بَعِيدٍ﴾ .(٢: ٢٥٢) ﴿وَإِنَّكَ لَمِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ﴾ .(٤: ١٥٧) ﴿وَإِنَّ الَّذِينَ اخْتَلَفُوا فِيهِ لَفِي شَكٍّ مِنْهُ﴾(٥: ٥٣) ﴿إِنَّهُمْ لَمَعَكُمْ﴾ .(٥: ١٠٦) ﴿إِنَّا إِذاً لَمِنَ الْآثِمِينَ﴾ .(٥: ١٠٧) ﴿إِنَّا إِذاً لَمِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ﴾ .(٧: ٢١) ﴿وَقَاسَمَهُمَا إِنِّي لَكُمَا لَمِنَ النَّاصِحِينَ﴾ .

**English**:
> (40: 37) ﴿The ways into the heavens - so that I may look at the deity of Moses; but indeed, I think he is a liar." And thus was made attractive to Pharaoh the evil of his deed, and he was averted from the right way. And the plan of Pharaoh was not except in ruin.﴾ (40: 51) ﴿Indeed, We will support Our messengers and those who maintain conviction in established truth during the life of this world and on the Day when the witnesses will stand -﴾ (41: 9) say your Aʾn disbelieve, deny who, which, the one who created the Earth in day (42: 52) ﴿And thus We have revealed to you an inspiration of Our command. You did not know what is the Inscribed Scripture (Al-Kitāb) or what is faith, but We have made it a light by which We guide whom We will of Our servants. And indeed, O Messenger, you guide to a Straight Path -﴾. (43: 37) ﴿And indeed, the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) avert them from the way of guidance while they think that they are rightly guided﴾. (53: 27) ﴿Indeed, those who do not believe in the Hereafter name the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) female names,﴾. (58: 2) ﴿Those who pronounce thihar among you to separate from their wives - they are not consequently their mothers. Their mothers are none but those who gave birth to them. And indeed, they are saying an objectionable statement and a falsehood. But indeed, Allāh is ever Pardoning and Forgiving.﴾. (69: 49) ﴿And indeed, We know that among you are deniers.﴾. (96: 6) ﴿No! But indeed, man transgresses﴾. (J) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon the Predicate (al-Khabar) resemble the sentence (al-jumlah): (2: 130) ﴿And who would be averse to the religion of Abraham except one who makes a fool of himself. And We had chosen him in this world, and indeed he, in the Hereafter, will be among the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār).﴾. (2: 145) ﴿And if you brought to those who were given the Inscribed Scripture every sign, they would not follow your qiblah. Nor will you be a follower of their qiblah. Nor would they be followers of one another's qiblah. So if you were to follow their desires after what has come to you of knowledge, indeed, you would then be among the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn).﴾. (2: 176) ﴿That is deserved by them because Allāh has sent down the Inscribed Scripture (Al-Kitāb) in truth. And indeed, those who differ over the Inscribed Scripture (Al-Kitāb) are in extreme dissension.﴾. (2: 252) ﴿These are the verses of Allāh which We recite to you, O Messenger, in truth. And indeed, you are from among the messengers.﴾. (4: 157) ﴿And for their assertion: 'Indeed, we killed the Messiah, Jesus, the son of Mary, the messenger of Allāh.' But they killed him not, nor did they crucify him, but so it was made to appear unto them. And indeed, those who differ concerning it are in doubt thereof; they have no verified knowledge thereof except the pursuit of conjecture. And they killed him not for certain!﴾ (5: 53) how with, together with you. (5: 106) ﴿O you who have believed, testimony should be taken among you when death approaches one of you at the time of bequest - that of two just men from among you or two others from outside if you are traveling through the land and the disaster of death should strike you. Detain them after the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) and let them both swear by Allāh if you doubt their testimony, saying, "We will not exchange our oath for a price, even if he should be a near relative, and we will not withhold the testimony of Allāh. Indeed, we would then be of the sinful."﴾. (5: 107) ﴿But if it is found that those two were guilty of perjury, let two others stand in their place who are foremost in claim from those who have a lawful right. And let them swear by Allāh, "Our testimony is truer than their testimony, and we have not transgressed. Indeed, we would then be of the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn)."﴾. (7: 21) ﴿And he swore by Allāh to them, "Indeed, I am to you from among the sincere advisors."﴾.

### Folio 23 (`Page ID: 11928-23` | `172` Words | `17` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٧: ١١٤) ﴿قَالَ نَعَمْ وَإِنَّكُمْ لَمِنَ الْمُقَرَّبِينَ﴾ .(٩: ٥٦) ﴿وَيَحْلِفُونَ بِاللَّهِ إِنَّهُمْ لَمِنْكُمْ﴾ .(١٠: ٨٣) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الْمُسْرِفِينَ﴾ .(١١: ٣١) ﴿إِنِّي إِذاً لَمِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ﴾ .(١١: ٦٢) ﴿وَإِنَّنَا لَفِي شَكٍّ مِمَّا تَدْعُونَا إِلَيْهِ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .(١١: ١١٠) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَفِي شَكٍّ مِنْهُ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .(١٢: ٨) ﴿إِنَّ أَبَانَا لَفِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(١٢: ٥١) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الصَّادِقِينَ﴾ .(١٢: ٩٥) ﴿قَالُوا تَاللَّهِ إِنَّكَ لَفِي ضَلالِكَ الْقَدِيمِ﴾ .(١٣: ٥) ﴿أَإِذَا كُنَّا تُرَاباً أَإِنَّا لَفِي خَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ﴾ .(١٤: ٩) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَفِي شَكٍّ مِمَّا تَدْعُونَنَا إِلَيْهِ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .(١٥: ٦٠) ﴿إِنَّهَا لَمِنَ الْغَابِرِينَ﴾ .(١٥: ٧٦) ﴿وَإِنَّهَا لَبِسَبِيلٍ مُقِيمٍ﴾ .(١٥: ٧٩) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمَا لَبِإِمَامٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(١٦: ١٢٢) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ فِي الْآخِرَةِ لَمِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ﴾(٢١: ٥٩) ﴿إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ﴾ .(٢٢: ٥٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ الظَّالِمِينَ لَفِي شِقَاقٍ بَعِيدٍ﴾(٢٢: ٦٧) ﴿إِنَّكَ لَعَلَى هُدىً مُسْتَقِيمٍ﴾(٢٤: ٦) ﴿فَشَهَادَةُ أَحَدِهِمْ أَرْبَعُ شَهَادَاتٍ بِاللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الصَّادِقِينَ﴾(٢٤: ٨) ﴿وَيَدْرَأُ عَنْهَا الْعَذَابَ أَنْ تَشْهَدَ أَرْبَعَ شَهَادَاتٍ بِاللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الْكَاذِبِينَ﴾(٢٦: ٤٢) ﴿قَالَ نَعَمْ وَإِنَّكُمْ إِذاً لَمِنَ الْمُقَرَّبِينَ﴾ .(٢٦: ١٩٦) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَفِي زُبُرِ الْأَوَّلِينَ﴾ .

**English**:
> (7: 114) ﴿He said, "Yes, and, moreover, you will be among those made near to me."﴾. (9: 56) ﴿And they swear by Allāh that they are from among you while they are not from among you; but they are a people who are afraid.﴾. (10: 83) ﴿But no one believed Moses, except some youths among his people, for fear of Pharaoh and his establishment that they would persecute them. And indeed, Pharaoh was haughty within the land, and indeed, he was of the transgressors﴾. (11: 31) ﴿And I do not tell you that I have the depositories containing the provision of Allāh or that I know the transcendent unmanifest realities (Al-Ghayb), nor do I tell you that I am an angel, nor do I say of those upon whom your eyes look down that Allāh shall never grant them any good. Allāh is ever most knowing of what is within their souls. Indeed, I would then be among the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn)."﴾. (11: 62) ﴿They said, "O Salih, you were among us a man of promise before this. Do you forbid us to worship what our fathers worshipped? And indeed we are, about that to which you invite us, in disquieting doubt."﴾. (11: 110) ﴿And We had certainly given Moses the Inscribed Scripture, but it came under disagreement. And if not for a word that preceded from your Sovereign Lord, it would have been judged between them. And indeed they are, concerning the Qur'an, in disquieting doubt.﴾. (12: 8) ﴿When they said, "Joseph and his brother are more beloved to our father than we, while we are a clan. Indeed, our father is in clear error.﴾. (12: 51) ﴿Said the king to the women, "What was your condition when you sought to seduce Joseph?" They said, "Perfect is Allāh! We know about him no evil." The wife of al-'Azeez said, "Now the truth has become evident. It was I who sought to seduce him, and indeed, he is of the truthful.﴾. (12: 95) ﴿They said, "By Allāh, indeed you are in your same old error."﴾. (13: 5) Aʾdh be dust, soil Aʾn in, within created new. (14: 9) ﴿Has there not reached you the news of those before you - the people of Noah and 'Aad and Thamud and those after them? No one knows them but Allāh. Their messengers brought them clear proofs, but they returned their hands to their mouths and said, "Indeed, we disbelieve in that with which you have been sent, and indeed we are, about that to which you invite us, in disquieting doubt."﴾. (15: 60) ﴿Except his wife." Allāh decreed that she is of those who remain behind.﴾. (15: 76) ﴿And indeed, those cities are situated on an established road.﴾. (15: 79) ﴿So We took retribution from them, and indeed, both cities are on a clear highway.﴾. (16: 122) ﴿And who would be averse to the religion of Abraham except one who makes a fool of himself. And We had chosen him in this world, and indeed he, in the Hereafter, will be among the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār).﴾ (21: 59) ﴿They said, "Who has done this to our gods? Indeed, he is of the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn)."﴾. (22: 53) ﴿That is so He may make what Satan (Ash-Shayṭān) throws in a trial for those within whose hearts is disease and those hard of heart. And indeed, the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) are in extreme dissension.﴾ (22: 67) ﴿For every religion We have appointed rites which they perform. So, O Messenger, let the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn) not contend with you over the matter but invite them to your Sovereign Lord. Indeed, you are upon straight guidance.﴾ (24: 6) ﴿And those who accuse their wives of adultery and have no witnesses except themselves - then the witness of one of them shall be four testimonies swearing by Allāh that indeed, he is of the truthful.﴾ (24: 8) ﴿But it will prevent punishment from her if she gives four testimonies swearing by Allāh that indeed, he is of the liars.﴾ (26: 42) ﴿He said, "Yes, and indeed, you will then be of those near to me."﴾. (26: 196) ﴿And indeed, it is mentioned in the scriptures of former peoples.﴾.

  **[S1] EN**: (7: 114) ﴿He said, "Yes, and, moreover, you will be among those made near to me."﴾.

  **[S2] EN**: (9: 56) ﴿And they swear by Allāh that they are from among you while they are not from among you; but they are a people who are afraid.﴾.
- **[S3] AR**: (١٠: ٨٣) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الْمُسْرِفِينَ﴾ .
  **[S3] EN**: (10: 83) ﴿But no one believed Moses, except some youths among his people, for fear of Pharaoh and his establishment that they would persecute them. And indeed, Pharaoh was haughty within the land, and indeed, he was of the transgressors﴾.

  **[S4] EN**: (11: 31) ﴿And I do not tell you that I have the depositories containing the provision of Allāh or that I know the transcendent unmanifest realities (Al-Ghayb), nor do I tell you that I am an angel, nor do I say of those upon whom your eyes look down that Allāh shall never grant them any good. Allāh is ever most knowing of what is within their souls. Indeed, I would then be among the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn)."﴾.
- **[S5] AR**: (١١: ٦٢) ﴿وَإِنَّنَا لَفِي شَكٍّ مِمَّا تَدْعُونَا إِلَيْهِ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .
  **[S5] EN**: (11: 62) ﴿They said, "O Salih, you were among us a man of promise before this. Do you forbid us to worship what our fathers worshipped? And indeed we are, about that to which you invite us, in disquieting doubt."﴾.
- **[S6] AR**: (١١: ١١٠) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَفِي شَكٍّ مِنْهُ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .
  **[S6] EN**: (11: 110) ﴿And We had certainly given Moses the Inscribed Scripture, but it came under disagreement. And if not for a word that preceded from your Sovereign Lord, it would have been judged between them. And indeed they are, concerning the Qur'an, in disquieting doubt.﴾.
- **[S7] AR**: (١٢: ٨) ﴿إِنَّ أَبَانَا لَفِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .
  **[S7] EN**: (12: 8) ﴿When they said, "Joseph and his brother are more beloved to our father than we, while we are a clan. Indeed, our father is in clear error.﴾.
- **[S8] AR**: (١٢: ٥١) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الصَّادِقِينَ﴾ .
  **[S8] EN**: (12: 51) ﴿Said the king to the women, "What was your condition when you sought to seduce Joseph?" They said, "Perfect is Allāh! We know about him no evil." The wife of al-'Azeez said, "Now the truth has become evident. It was I who sought to seduce him, and indeed, he is of the truthful.﴾.
- **[S9] AR**: (١٢: ٩٥) ﴿قَالُوا تَاللَّهِ إِنَّكَ لَفِي ضَلالِكَ الْقَدِيمِ﴾ .
  **[S9] EN**: (12: 95) ﴿They said, "By Allāh, indeed you are in your same old error."﴾.
- **[S10] AR**: (١٣: ٥) ﴿أَإِذَا كُنَّا تُرَاباً أَإِنَّا لَفِي خَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ﴾ .
  **[S10] EN**: (13: 5) Aʾdh be dust, soil Aʾn in, within created new.
- **[S11] AR**: (١٤: ٩) ﴿وَإِنَّا لَفِي شَكٍّ مِمَّا تَدْعُونَنَا إِلَيْهِ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .
  **[S11] EN**: (14: 9) ﴿Has there not reached you the news of those before you - the people of Noah and 'Aad and Thamud and those after them? No one knows them but Allāh.   **[S13] EN**: (15: 76) ﴿And indeed, those cities are situated on an established road.﴾.

  **[S14] EN**: (15: 79) ﴿So We took retribution from them, and indeed, both cities are on a clear highway.﴾.
- **[S15] AR**: (١٦: ١٢٢) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ فِي الْآخِرَةِ لَمِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ﴾ (٢١: ٥٩) ﴿إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ﴾ .

- **[S16] AR**: (٢٢: ٥٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ الظَّالِمِينَ لَفِي شِقَاقٍ بَعِيدٍ﴾ (٢٢: ٦٧) ﴿إِنَّكَ لَعَلَى هُدىً مُسْتَقِيمٍ﴾ (٢٤: ٦) ﴿فَشَهَادَةُ أَحَدِهِمْ أَرْبَعُ شَهَادَاتٍ بِاللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الصَّادِقِينَ﴾ (٢٤: ٨) ﴿وَيَدْرَأُ عَنْهَا الْعَذَابَ أَنْ تَشْهَدَ أَرْبَعَ شَهَادَاتٍ بِاللَّهِ إِنَّهُ لَمِنَ الْكَاذِبِينَ﴾ (٢٦: ٤٢) ﴿قَالَ نَعَمْ وَإِنَّكُمْ إِذاً لَمِنَ الْمُقَرَّبِينَ﴾ .
  **[S16] EN**: (22: 53) ﴿That is so He may make what Satan (Ash-Shayṭān) throws in a trial for those within whose hearts is disease and those hard of heart. So, O Messenger, let the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn) not contend with you over the matter but invite them to your Sovereign Lord. ### Folio 24 (`Page ID: 11928-24` | `181` Words | `20` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٢٩: ٢٧) ﴿وَإِنَّهُ فِي الْآخِرَةِ لَمِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ﴾ .(٢٩: ٦٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَمَعَ الْمُحْسِنِينَ﴾ .(٣٤: ٧) ﴿إِذَا مُزِّقْتُمْ كُلَّ مُمَزَّقٍ إِنَّكُمْ لَفِي خَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ﴾(٣٤: ٢٤) ﴿وَإِنَّا أَوْ إِيَّاكُمْ لَعَلَى هُدىً أَوْ فِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(٣٦: ٣) ﴿إِنَّكَ لَمِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ﴾ .(٣٦: ٢٤) ﴿إِنِّي إِذاً لَفِي ضَلالٍ مُبِينٍ﴾ .(٣٧: ٥٢) ﴿يَقُولُ أَإِنَّكَ لَمِنَ الْمُصَدِّقِينَ﴾ .(٣٧: ٦٨) ﴿ثُمَّ إِنَّ مَرْجِعَهُمْ لَإِلَى الْجَحِيمِ﴾ .(٣٧: ١٢٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ إِلْيَاسَ لَمِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ﴾ .(٣٧: ١٣٣) ﴿وَإِنَّ لُوطاً لَمِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ﴾ .(٣٧: ١٣٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ يُونُسَ لَمِنَ الْمُرْسَلِينَ﴾ .(٣٨: ٤٧) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ عِنْدَنَا لَمِنَ الْمُصْطَفَيْنَ الْأَخْيَارِ﴾ .(٤١: ٤٥) ﴿وَإِنَّهُمْ لَفِي شَكٍّ مِنْهُ مُرِيبٍ﴾ .(٤٢: ١٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ الَّذِينَ أُورِثُوا الْكِتَابَ مِنْ بَعْدِهِمْ لَفِي شَكٍّ مِنْهُ مُرِيبٍ﴾(٤٢: ١٨) ﴿َلا إِنَّ الَّذِينَ يُمَارُونَ فِي السَّاعَةِ لَفِي ضَلالٍ بَعِيدٍ﴾ .(٤٢: ٤٣) ﴿إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَمِنْ عَزْمِ الْأُمُورِ﴾ .(٥١: ٨) ﴿إِنَّكُمْ لَفِي قَوْلٍ مُخْتَلِفٍ﴾ .(٥٤: ٢٤) ﴿إِنَّا إِذاً لَفِي ضَلالٍ وَسُعُرٍ﴾ .(٨٢: ١٣) ﴿إِنَّ الْأَبْرَارَ لَفِي نَعِيمٍ﴾ .(٨٢: ١٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ الْفُجَّارَ لَفِي جَحِيمٍ﴾ .(٨٣: ٧) ﴿كَلَّا إِنَّ كِتَابَ الْفُجَّارِ لَفِي سِجِّينٍ﴾ .(٨٣: ١٨) ﴿كَلَّا إِنَّ كِتَابَ الْأَبْرَارِ لَفِي عِلِّيِّينَ﴾

**English**:
> (29: 27) ﴿And who would be averse to the religion of Abraham except one who makes a fool of himself. And We had chosen him in this world, and indeed he, in the Hereafter, will be among the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār).﴾. (29: 69) ﴿And those who strive for Us - We will surely guide them to Our ways. And indeed, Allāh is ever with the doers of good.﴾. (34: 7) ﴿But those who stubbornly reject established reality (Al-Kāfirūn) say, "Shall we direct you to a man who will inform you that when you have disintegrated in complete disintegration, you will then be recreated in a new creation?﴾ (34: 24) ﴿Say, "Who provides for you from the heavens and the terrestrial earth?" Say, "Allāh. And indeed, we or you are either upon guidance or in clear error."﴾. (36: 3) ﴿Indeed you, O Messenger, are from among the messengers,﴾. (36: 24) ﴿Indeed, I would then be in manifest error.﴾. (37: 52) says your Aʾn to whoever confirming. (37: 68) ﴿Then indeed, their return will be to the Hellfire.﴾. (37: 123) ﴿And indeed, Elias was from among the messengers,﴾. (37: 133) ﴿And indeed, Lot was among the messengers.﴾. (37: 139) ﴿And indeed, Jonah was among the messengers.﴾. (38: 47) ﴿And indeed they are, to Us, among the chosen and outstanding.﴾. (41: 45) ﴿And We had certainly given Moses the Inscribed Scripture, but it came under disagreement. And if not for a word that preceded from your Sovereign Lord, it would have been judged between them. And indeed they are, concerning the Qur'an, in disquieting doubt.﴾. (42: 14) ﴿And they did not become divided until after knowledge had come to them - out of jealous animosity between themselves. And if not for a word that preceded from your Sovereign Lord postponing the penalty until a specified time, it would have been concluded between them. And indeed, those who were granted inheritance of the Inscribed Scripture after them are, concerning it, in disquieting doubt.﴾ (42: 18) ﴿Those who do not believe in it are impatient for it, but those who maintain conviction in established truth are fearful of it and know that it is the truth. Unquestionably, those who dispute concerning the Hour are in extreme error.﴾. (42: 43) ﴿And whoever is patient and forgives - indeed, that is of the matters requiring determination.﴾. (51: 8) ﴿Indeed, you are in differing speech.﴾. (54: 24) ﴿And said, "Is it one human being among us that we should follow? Indeed, we would then be in error and madness.﴾. (82: 13) ﴿Indeed, the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) will be in pleasure,﴾. (82: 14) ﴿And indeed, the wicked will be in Hellfire.﴾. (83: 7) ﴿No! Indeed, the record of the wicked is in sijjeen.﴾. (83: 18) ﴿No! Indeed, the record of the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) is in 'illiyyun.﴾

### Folio 25 (`Page ID: 11928-25` | `172` Words | `16` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٨٣: ٢٢) ﴿إِنَّ الْأَبْرَارَ لَفِي نَعِيمٍ﴾ .(٨٧: ١٨) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَفِي الصُّحُفِ الْأُولَى﴾ .(٨٩: ١٤) ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَبِالْمِرْصَادِ﴾ .(١٠٣: ٢) ﴿إِنَّ الْأِنْسَانَ لَفِي خُسْرٍ﴾(د) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على اسم إنَّ المؤخر:(٢: ١٦٤) ﴿إِنَّ فِي خَلْقِ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ وَاخْتِلافِ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ لَآياتٍ لِأُولِي الْأَلْبَابِ﴾ .(٢: ٢٤٨) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .(٣: ١٣) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَعِبْرَةً﴾ .(٣: ٤٩) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .(٣: ٧٨) ﴿وَإِنَّ مِنْهُمْ لَفَرِيقاً يَلْوُونَ أَلْسِنَتَهُمْ بِالْكِتَابِ﴾ .(٣: ١٩٠) ﴿إِنَّ فِي خَلْقِ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ وَاخْتِلافِ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ لَآياتٍ لِأُولِي الْأَلْبَابِ﴾(٤: ٧٢) ﴿وَإِنَّ مِنْكُمْ لَمَنْ لَيُبَطِّئَنَّ﴾ .(٦: ٩٩) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكُمْ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(٧: ١١٣) ﴿قَالُوا إِنَّ لَنَا لَأَجْراً إِنْ كُنَّا نَحْنُ الْغَالِبِينَ﴾ .(١٠: ٦) ﴿إِنَّ فِي اخْتِلافِ اللَّيْلِ وَالنَّهَارِ وَمَا خَلَقَ اللَّهُ فِي السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضِ لَآياتٍ﴾(١٠: ٦٧) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(١١: ١٠٣) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .(١٣: ٣) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(١٣: ٤) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(١٤: ٥) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾(١٥: ٧٢) ﴿لعمرك إنهم لفي سكرتهم يعمهون﴾

**English**:
> (83: 22) ﴿Indeed, the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) will be in pleasure,﴾. (87: 18) ﴿Indeed, this is in the former scriptures,﴾. (89: 14) ﴿Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is in observation.﴾. (103: 2) ﴿Indeed, mankind is in loss,﴾ (D) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon noun Indeed the last: (2: 164) ﴿Indeed, in the creation of the heavens and the terrestrial earth and the alternation of the night and the day are signs for those of understanding.﴾. (2: 248) ﴿And their prophet said to them, "Indeed, a sign of his kingship is that the chest will come to you in which is assurance from your Sovereign Lord and a remnant of what the family of Moses and the family of Aaron had left, carried by the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah). Indeed in that is a sign for you, if you are believers."﴾. (3: 13) ﴿Already there has been for you a sign in the two armies which met - one fighting in the cause of Allāh and another of disbelievers. They saw them to be twice their own number by their eyesight. But Allāh supports with His victory whom He wills. Indeed in that is a lesson for those of vision.﴾. (3: 49) ﴿And their prophet said to them, "Indeed, a sign of his kingship is that the chest will come to you in which is assurance from your Sovereign Lord and a remnant of what the family of Moses and the family of Aaron had left, carried by the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah). Indeed in that is a sign for you, if you are believers."﴾. (3: 78) ﴿And indeed, there is among them a party who alter the Inscribed Scripture with their tongues so you may think it is from the Inscribed Scripture, but it is not from the Inscribed Scripture. And they say, "This is from Allāh," but it is not from Allāh. And they speak untruth about Allāh while they know.﴾. (3: 190) ﴿Indeed, in the creation of the heavens and the terrestrial earth and the alternation of the night and the day are signs for those of understanding.﴾ (4: 72) ﴿And indeed, there is among you he who lingers behind; and if disaster strikes you, he says, "Allāh has favored me in that I was not present with them."﴾. (6: 99) ﴿And it is He who sends down rain from the sky, and We produce thereby the growth of all things. We produce from it greenery from which We produce grains arranged in layers. And from the palm trees - of its emerging fruit are clusters hanging low. And We produce gardens of grapevines and olives and pomegranates, similar yet varied. Look at each of its fruit when it yields and at its ripening. Indeed in that are signs for a people who believe.﴾. (7: 113) ﴿And the magicians came to Pharaoh. They said, "Indeed for us is a reward if we are the predominant."﴾. (10: 6) ﴿Indeed, in the alternation of the night and the day and in what Allāh has created in the heavens and the terrestrial earth are signs for a people who fear Allāh﴾ (10: 67) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾. (11: 103) ﴿And their prophet said to them, "Indeed, a sign of his kingship is that the chest will come to you in which is assurance from your Sovereign Lord and a remnant of what the family of Moses and the family of Aaron had left, carried by the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah). Indeed in that is a sign for you, if you are believers."﴾. (13: 3) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾. (13: 4) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾. (14: 5) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾ (15: 72) ﴿By your life, O Messenger, indeed they were, in their intoxication, wandering blindly.﴾

### Folio 26 (`Page ID: 11928-26` | `169` Words | `18` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (١٥: ٧٥) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾(١٥: ٧٥) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ لِلْمُتَوَسِّمِينَ﴾(١٥: ٧٧) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ﴾ .(١٦: ١١) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَتَفَكَّرُونَ﴾ .(١٦: ١٢) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ لِقَوْمٍ يَعْقِلُونَ﴾ .(١٦: ١٣) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَذَّكَّرُونَ﴾ .(١٦: ٦٥) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَسْمَعُونَ﴾ .(١٦: ٦٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ لَكُمْ فِي الْأَنْعَامِ لَعِبْرَةً﴾ .(١٦: ٦٧) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .(١٦: ٦٩) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .(١٦: ٧٩) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(٢٠: ٥٤) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ﴾ .(٢٠: ١٢٨) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ لِأُولِي النُّهَى﴾ .(هـ) اللام المزحلقة الداخلة على ضمير الفصل:(٣: ٦٢) ﴿إِنَّ هَذَا لَهُوَ الْقَصَصُ الْحَقُّ﴾ .(٣: ٦٢) ﴿وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَهُوَ الْعَزِيزُ الْحَكِيمُ﴾ .(١١: ٨٧) ﴿إِنَّكَ لَأَنْتَ الْحَلِيمُ الرَّشِيدُ﴾ .(١٢: ٩٠) ﴿أَإِنَّكَ لَأَنْتَ يُوسُفُ﴾(٢٢: ٥٨) ﴿وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَهُوَ خَيْرُ الرَّازِقِينَ﴾ .(٢٢: ٦٤) ﴿وَإِنَّ اللَّهَ لَهُوَ الْغَنِيُّ الْحَمِيدُ﴾ .(٢٦: ٩) ﴿وَإِنَّ رَبَّكَ لَهُوَ الْعَزِيزُ الرَّحِيمُ﴾ .(٢٦: ٤٤) ﴿وَقَالُوا بِعِزَّةِ فِرْعَوْنَ إِنَّا لَنَحْنُ الْغَالِبُونَ﴾

**English**:
> (15: 75) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾ (15: 75) ﴿Indeed in that are signs for those who discern.﴾ (15: 77) ﴿Indeed in that is a sign for those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn).﴾. (16: 11) ﴿He causes to grow for you thereby the crops, olives, palm trees, grapevines, and from all the fruits. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who give thought.﴾. (16: 12) ﴿And within the land are neighboring plots and gardens of grapevines and crops and palm trees, growing several from a root or otherwise, watered with one water; but We make some of them exceed others in quality of fruit. Indeed in that are signs for a people who reason.﴾. (16: 13) ﴿And He has subjected whatever He multiplied for you on the terrestrial earth of varying colors. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who remember.﴾. (16: 65) ﴿And Allāh has sent down rain from the sky and given life thereby to the terrestrial earth after its lifelessness. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who listen.﴾. (16: 66) ﴿And indeed, for you in grazing livestock is a lesson. We give you drink from what is in their bellies - between excretion and blood - pure milk, palatable to drinkers.﴾. (16: 67) ﴿And their prophet said to them, "Indeed, a sign of his kingship is that the chest will come to you in which is assurance from your Sovereign Lord and a remnant of what the family of Moses and the family of Aaron had left, carried by the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah). Indeed in that is a sign for you, if you are believers."﴾. (16: 69) ﴿And their prophet said to them, "Indeed, a sign of his kingship is that the chest will come to you in which is assurance from your Sovereign Lord and a remnant of what the family of Moses and the family of Aaron had left, carried by the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah). Indeed in that is a sign for you, if you are believers."﴾. (16: 79) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾. (20: 54) ﴿It is He who made for you the night to rest therein and the day, giving sight. Indeed in that are signs for a people who listen.﴾. (20: 128) ﴿Eat therefrom and pasture your livestock. Indeed, in that are signs for those of intelligence.﴾. (AH) the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) the Mazḥalqah the enter upon a pronoun (ḍamīr) Chapter: (3: 62) ﴿Indeed, this is the true narration. And there is no deity except Allāh. And indeed, Allāh is ever the Almighty, the Supreme in Wisdom.﴾. (3: 62) ﴿Indeed, this is the true narration. And there is no deity except Allāh. And indeed, Allāh is ever the Almighty, the Supreme in Wisdom.﴾. (11: 87) ﴿They said, "O Shu'ayb, does your the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) command you that we should leave what our fathers worship or not do with our wealth what we please? Indeed, you are the forbearing, the discerning!"﴾. (12: 90) your Aʾn for you Yūsuf (22: 58) ﴿And those who emigrated for the cause of Allāh and then were killed or died - Allāh shall surely provide for them a good provision. And indeed, it is Allāh who is the best of providers.﴾. (22: 64) ﴿To Him belongs what is in the heavens and what is on the terrestrial earth. And indeed, Allāh is ever the Free of need, the Praiseworthy.﴾. (26: 9) ﴿And indeed, your Sovereign Lord - He is the Exalted in Might, the Merciful.﴾. (26: 44) ﴿So they threw their ropes and their staffs and said, "By the might of Pharaoh, indeed it is we who are predominant."﴾

- **[S2] AR**: (١٦: ١١) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَتَفَكَّرُونَ﴾ .
  **[S2] EN**: (16: 11) ﴿He causes to grow for you thereby the crops, olives, palm trees, grapevines, and from all the fruits. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who give thought.﴾.
- **[S3] AR**: (١٦: ١٢) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآياتٍ لِقَوْمٍ يَعْقِلُونَ﴾ .
  **[S3] EN**: (16: 12) ﴿And within the land are neighboring plots and gardens of grapevines and crops and palm trees, growing several from a root or otherwise, watered with one water; but We make some of them exceed others in quality of fruit. Indeed in that are signs for a people who reason.﴾.
- **[S4] AR**: (١٦: ١٣) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَذَّكَّرُونَ﴾ .
  **[S4] EN**: (16: 13) ﴿And He has subjected whatever He multiplied for you on the terrestrial earth of varying colors. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who remember.﴾.
- **[S5] AR**: (١٦: ٦٥) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً لِقَوْمٍ يَسْمَعُونَ﴾ .
  **[S5] EN**: (16: 65) ﴿And Allāh has sent down rain from the sky and given life thereby to the terrestrial earth after its lifelessness. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who listen.﴾.
- **[S6] AR**: (١٦: ٦٦) ﴿وَإِنَّ لَكُمْ فِي الْأَنْعَامِ لَعِبْرَةً﴾ .
  **[S6] EN**: (16: 66) ﴿And indeed, for you in grazing livestock is a lesson. We give you drink from what is in their bellies - between excretion and blood - pure milk, palatable to drinkers.﴾.
- **[S7] AR**: (١٦: ٦٧) ﴿إِنَّ فِي ذَلِكَ لَآيَةً﴾ .

**Arabic**:
> اللام الموطئة للقسم: هي اللام الداخلة على أداة شرط للإيذان بأن الجواب بعدها مبنيّ على قسم قبلها، لا على الشرط، ومن ثم تسمى اللام الموطئة للقسم لأنها وطَّأت الجواب للقسم أي مهَّدت له؛ وأكثر ما تدخل على» إنْ «؛ وقد تدخل على غيرها، نحو:» منْ «، فقد جاء في القرآن الكريم: ﴿لَمَنْ تَبِعَكَ مِنْهُمْ لَأَمْلَأَنَّ جَهَنَّمَ مِنْكُمْ أَجْمَعِينَ﴾ [الأعراف ١٨] .ومن ثم يشترط في جواب» لئنْ «ما يشترط في جواب القسم، وهي:١) إن كان الجواب جملة اسمية أكد بـ» إنَّ «واللام، أو بإحداهما.٢) وإن كان جملة فعلية مثبتة مصدرة بمضارع مفيد للاستقبال أكد باللام والنون؛ وإن كان مفيداً للحال فباللام فقط، وإن كان مفصولاً عن اللام لم يؤكد.٣) وإن صدِّرت بماضٍ أكد باللام وقد.٤) وإن كان جملة فعلية منفية لم يؤكد، ونفي بـ» ما «، أو» لا «، أو» إنْ «.٥) وإن كان جملة اسمية منفية لم يؤكد.

### Folio 30 (`Page ID: 11928-30` | `184` Words | `10` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (أ) الجواب جملة اسمية مثبة مؤكدة بإنَّ واللام:(٧: ٩٠) ﴿لَئِنِ اتَّبَعْتُمْ شُعَيْباً إِنَّكُمْ إِذاً لَخَاسِرُونَ﴾ .(١١: ٩) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَذَقْنَا الْأِنْسَانَ مِنَّا رَحْمَةً ثُمَّ نَزَعْنَاهَا مِنْهُ إِنَّهُ لَيَؤُوسٌ كَفُورٌ﴾(١١: ١٠) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَذَقْنَاهُ نَعْمَاءَ بَعْدَ ضَرَّاءَ مَسَّتْهُ لَيَقُولَنَّ ذَهَبَ السَّيِّئَاتُ عَنِّي إِنَّهُ لَفَرِحٌ فَخُورٌ﴾ .(١٢: ١٤) ﴿قَالُوا لَئِنْ أَكَلَهُ الذِّئْبُ وَنَحْنُ عُصْبَةٌ إِنَّا إِذاً لَخَاسِرُونَ﴾ .(١٤: ٧) ﴿وَلَئِنْ كَفَرْتُمْ إِنَّ عَذَابِي لَشَدِيدٌ﴾ .(٢٣: ٣٤) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَطَعْتُمْ بَشَراً مِثْلَكُمْ إِنَّكُمْ إِذاً لَخَاسِرُونَ﴾ .(٤١: ٥٠) ﴿وَلَئِنْ رُجِعْتُ إِلَى رَبِّي إِنَّ لِي عِنْدَهُ لَلْحُسْنَى﴾ ...(ب) الجواب جملة اسمية مثبتة مؤكدة باللام فقط:(٣: ١٥٧) ﴿وَلَئِنْ قُتِلْتُمْ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ أَوْ مُتُّمْ لَمَغْفِرَةٌ مِنَ اللَّهِ وَرَحْمَةٌ خَيْرٌ مِمَّا يَجْمَعُونَ﴾(١٦: ١٢٦) ﴿وَلَئِنْ صَبَرْتُمْ لَهُوَ خَيْرٌ لِلصَّابِرِينَ﴾ .(ج) الجواب جملة اسمية منفية:(٢: ١٢٠) ﴿وَلَئِنِ اتَّبَعْتَ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ بَعْدَ الَّذِي جَاءَكَ مِنَ الْعِلْمِ مَا لَكَ مِنَ اللَّهِ مِنْ وَلِيٍّ وَلا نَصِيرٍ﴾ .(٥: ٢٨) ﴿لَئِنْ بَسَطْتَ إِلَيَّ يَدَكَ لِتَقْتُلَنِي مَا أَنَا بِبَاسِطٍ يَدِيَ إِلَيْكَ لِأَقْتُلَكَ﴾ .(١٣: ٣٧) ﴿وَلَئِنِ اتَّبَعْتَ أَهْوَاءَهُمْ بَعْدَ مَا جَاءَكَ مِنَ الْعِلْمِ مَا لَكَ مِنَ اللَّهِ مِنْ وَلِيٍّ وَلا وَاقٍ﴾(د) الجواب جملة فعلية فعلها ماضٍ مثبت أكد باللام:

**English**:
> (whether) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) name Mathabah emphasize that and the letter Lām: (7: 90) ﴿Said the eminent ones who disbelieved among his people, "If you should follow Shu'ayb, indeed, you would then be losers."﴾. (11: 9) ﴿And if We give man a taste of mercy from Us and then We withdraw it from him, indeed, he is despairing and ungrateful.﴾ (11: 10) ﴿But if We give him a taste of favor after hardship has touched him, he will surely say, "Bad times have left me." Indeed, he is exultant and boastful -﴾. (12: 14) ﴿They said, "If a wolf should eat him while we are a strong clan, indeed, we would then be losers."﴾. (14: 7) ﴿And remember when your Sovereign Lord proclaimed, 'If you are grateful, I will surely increase you in favor; but if you deny, indeed, My punishment is severe.' "﴾. (23: 34) ﴿And if you should obey a man like yourselves, indeed, you would then be losers.﴾. (41: 50) ﴿And if We let him taste mercy from Us after an adversity which has touched him, he will surely say, "This is to me, and I do not think the Hour will occur; and even if I should be returned to my Sovereign Lord, indeed, for me there will be with Him the best." But We will surely inform those who rejected established reality about what they did, and We will surely make them taste a massive punishment.﴾... (B) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) name plant emphasize in the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm) only (faqaṭ): (3: 157) ﴿And if you are killed in the cause of Allāh or die - then forgiveness from Allāh and mercy are better than whatever they accumulate in this world.﴾ (16: 126) ﴿And if you punish an enemy, O believers, punish with an equivalent of that with which you were harmed. But if you are patient - it is better for those who are patient.﴾. (J) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) name Manfiyyah: (2: 120) ﴿And never will the Jews or the Christians approve of you until you follow their religion. Say, "Indeed, the guidance of Allāh is ever the only guidance." If you were to follow their desires after what has come to you of knowledge, you would have against Allāh no protector or helper.﴾. (5: 28) ﴿If you should raise your hand against me to kill me - I shall not raise my hand against you to kill you. Indeed, I fear Allāh, Sovereign Sustainer and Evolver of all realms of existence.﴾. (13: 37) ﴿And thus We have revealed it as an Arabic legislation. And if you should follow their inclinations after what has come to you of knowledge, you would not have against Allāh any ally or any protector.﴾ (D) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) do action, deed her past verb (māḍin) plant more emphatic strongly confirmed (ākad) in the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm):

### Folio 31 (`Page ID: 11928-31` | `178` Words | `8` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٣٠: ٥١) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَرْسَلْنَا رِيحاً فَرَأَوْهُ مُصْفَرّاً لَظَلُّوا مِنْ بَعْدِهِ يَكْفُرُونَ﴾ .(هـ) الجواب جملة فعلية فعلها ماضٍ منفي:(٣٥: ٤١) ﴿وَلَئِنْ زَالَتَا إِنْ أَمْسَكَهُمَا مِنْ أَحَدٍ مِنْ بَعْدِهِ﴾(و) الجواب جملة فعلية فعلها مضارع مستقبل مثبت متصل باللام:(٤: ٧٣) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَصَابَكُمْ فَضْلٌ مِنَ اللَّهِ لَيَقُولَنَّ كَأَنْ لَمْ تَكُنْ بَيْنَكُمْ وَبَيْنَهُ مَوَدَّةٌ﴾ .(٥: ١٢) ﴿لَئِنْ أَقَمْتُمُ الصَّلاةَ وَآتَيْتُمُ الزَّكَاةَ وَآمَنْتُمْ بِرُسُلِي وَعَزَّرْتُمُوهُمْ وَأَقْرَضْتُمُ اللَّهَ قَرْضاً حَسَناً لَأُكَفِّرَنَّ عَنْكُمْ سَيِّئَاتِكُمْ وَلَأُدْخِلَنَّكُمْ جَنَّاتٍ تَجْرِي مِنْ تَحْتِهَا الْأَنْهَارُ﴾ .(٦: ٦٣) ﴿لَئِنْ أَنْجَانَا مِنْ هَذِهِ لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الشَّاكِرِينَ﴾(٦: ٧٧) ﴿فَلَمَّا أَفَلَ قَالَ لَئِنْ لَمْ يَهْدِنِي رَبِّي لَأَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْقَوْمِ الضَّالِّينَ﴾ .(٦: ١٠٩) ﴿وَأَقْسَمُوا بِاللَّهِ جَهْدَ أَيْمَانِهِمْ لَئِنْ جَاءَتْهُمْ آيَةٌ لَيُؤْمِنُنَّ بِهَا﴾(٧: ١٣٤) ﴿لَئِنْ كَشَفْتَ عَنَّا الرِّجْزَ لَنُؤْمِنَنَّ لَكَ وَلَنُرْسِلَنَّ مَعَكَ بَنِي إِسْرائيلَ﴾ .(٧: ١٤٩) ﴿لَئِنْ لَمْ يَرْحَمْنَا رَبُّنَا وَيَغْفِرْ لَنَا لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْخَاسِرِينَ﴾ .(٧: ١٨٩) ﴿لَئِنْ آتَيْتَنَا صَالِحاً لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الشَّاكِرِينَ﴾ .(٩: ٦٥) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ لَيَقُولُنَّ إِنَّمَا كُنَّا نَخُوضُ وَنَلْعَبُ﴾(٩: ٧٥) ﴿وَمِنْهُمْ مَنْ عَاهَدَ اللَّهَ لَئِنْ آتَانَا مِنْ فَضْلِهِ لَنَصَّدَّقَنَّ وَلَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الصَّالِحِينَ﴾(١٠: ٢٢) ﴿لَئِنْ أَنْجَيْتَنَا مِنْ هَذِهِ لَنَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الشَّاكِرِينَ﴾

**English**:
> (30: 51) ﴿But if We should send a bad wind and they saw their crops turned yellow, they would remain thereafter disbelievers.﴾. (AH) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) do action, deed her past verb (māḍin) negated: (35: 41) ﴿Indeed, Allāh holds the heavens and the terrestrial earth, lest they cease. And if they should cease, no one could hold them in place after Him. Indeed, He is Forbearing and Forgiving.﴾ (and) the answer (al-jawāb) a sentence (jumlah) do action, deed her imperfect verb (muḍāriʿ) before plant connect in the uterine [by the mother] (li-umm): (4: 73) ﴿But if bounty comes to you from Allāh, he will surely say, as if there had never been between you and him any affection. "Oh, I wish I had been with them so I could have attained a great attainment."﴾. (5: 12) ﴿And Allāh had already taken a solemn covenant (Mīthāq) from the Children of Israel, and We delegated from among them twelve leaders. And Allāh said, "I am with you. If you establish the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) and give the purifying equity of Zakāh and believe in My messengers and support them and loan Allāh a goodly loan, I will surely remove from you your misdeeds and admit you to Gardens of Perpetual Bliss beneath which rivers flow in continuous renewal. But whoever of you disbelieves after that has certainly strayed from the soundness of the way."﴾. (6: 63) if our Anj from this be from grateful, thankful (6: 77) ﴿And when he saw the moon rising, he said, "This is my Sovereign Lord." But when it set, he said, "Unless my Sovereign Lord guides me, I will surely be among the people gone astray."﴾. (6: 109) ﴿And they swear by Allāh their strongest oaths that if a sign came to them, they would surely believe in it. Say, "The signs are only with Allāh." And what will make you perceive that even if a sign came, they would not believe.﴾ (7: 134) ﴿And when the punishment descended upon them, they said, "O Moses, invoke for us your Sovereign Lord by what He has promised you. If you can remove the punishment from us, we will surely believe you, and we will send with you the Children of Israel."﴾. (7: 149) ﴿And when regret overcame them and they saw that they had gone astray, they said, "If our Sovereign Lord does not have mercy upon us and forgive us, we will surely be among the losers."﴾. (7: 189) ﴿It is He who created you from one soul and created from it its mate that he might dwell in security with her. And when he covers her, she carries a light burden and continues therein. And when it becomes heavy, they both invoke Allāh, their Sovereign Lord, "If You should give us a good child, we will surely be among the grateful."﴾. (9: 65) ﴿And if you ask them, they will surely say, "We were only conversing and playing." Say, "Is it Allāh and His verses and His Messenger that you were mocking?"﴾ (9: 75) ﴿And among them are those who made a solemn covenant (Mīthāq) with Allāh, saying, "If He should give us from His bounty, we will surely spend in charity, and we will surely be among the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār)."﴾ (10: 22) ﴿It is He who enables you to travel on land and sea until, when you are in ships and they sail with them by a good wind and they rejoice therein, there comes a storm wind and the waves come upon them from everywhere and they assume that they are surrounded, supplicating Allāh, sincere to Him in religion, "If You should save us from this, we will surely be among the thankful."﴾
