### Folio 32 (`Page ID: 11928-32` | `203` Words | `9` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (١١: ٧) ﴿وَلَئِنْ قُلْتَ إِنَّكُمْ مَبْعُوثُونَ مِنْ بَعْدِ الْمَوْتِ لَيَقُولَنَّ الَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا إِنْ هَذَا إِلَّا سِحْرٌ مُبِينٌ﴾(١١: ٨) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَخَّرْنَا عَنْهُمُ الْعَذَابَ إِلَى أُمَّةٍ مَعْدُودَةٍ لَيَقُولُنَّ مَا يَحْبِسُهُ﴾ .(١١: ١٠) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَذَقْنَاهُ نَعْمَاءَ بَعْدَ ضَرَّاءَ مَسَّتْهُ لَيَقُولَنَّ ذَهَبَ السَّيِّئَاتُ عَنِّي﴾ .(١٢: ٣٢) ﴿وَلَئِنْ لَمْ يَفْعَلْ مَا آمُرُهُ لَيُسْجَنَنَّ وَلِيَكُوناً مِنَ الصَّاغِرِينَ﴾ .(١٤: ٧) ﴿وَإِذْ تَأَذَّنَ رَبُّكُمْ لَئِنْ شَكَرْتُمْ لَأَزِيدَنَّكُمْ﴾ .(١٧: ٦٢) ﴿لَئِنْ أَخَّرْتَنِ إِلَى يَوْمِ الْقِيَامَةِ لَأَحْتَنِكَنَّ ذُرِّيَّتَهُ إِلَّا قَلِيلاً﴾(١٧: ٨٦) ﴿وَلَئِنْ شِئْنَا لَنَذْهَبَنَّ بِالَّذِي أَوْحَيْنَا إِلَيْكَ ثُمَّ لا تَجِدُ لَكَ بِهِ عَلَيْنَا وَكِيلاً﴾(١٨: ٣٦) ﴿وَلَئِنْ رُدِدْتُ إِلَى رَبِّي لَأَجِدَنَّ خَيْراً مِنْهَا مُنْقَلَباً﴾(١٩: ٤٦) ﴿لَئِنْ لَمْ تَنْتَهِ لَأَرْجُمَنَّكَ﴾(٢١: ٤٦) ﴿وَلَئِنْ مَسَّتْهُمْ نَفْحَةٌ مِنْ عَذَابِ رَبِّكَ لَيَقُولُنَّ يَا وَيْلَنَا إِنَّا كُنَّا ظَالِمِينَ﴾ .(٢٤: ٥٣) ﴿لَئِنْ أَمَرْتَهُمْ لَيَخْرُجُنَّ﴾(٢٦: ٢٩) ﴿قَالَ لَئِنِ اتَّخَذْتَ إِلَهاً غَيْرِي لَأَجْعَلَنَّكَ مِنَ الْمَسْجُونِينَ﴾(٢٦: ١١٦) ﴿قَالُوا لَئِنْ لَمْ تَنْتَهِ يَا نُوحُ لَتَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْمَرْجُومِينَ﴾(٢٦: ١٦٧) ﴿قَالُوا لَئِنْ لَمْ تَنْتَهِ يَا لُوطُ لَتَكُونَنَّ مِنَ الْمُخْرَجِينَ﴾ .(٢٩: ١٠) ﴿وَلَئِنْ جَاءَ نَصْرٌ مِنْ رَبِّكَ لَيَقُولُنَّ إِنَّا كُنَّا مَعَكُمْ﴾ .(٢٩: ٦١) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ وَسَخَّرَ الشَّمْسَ وَالْقَمَرَ لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾ .(٢٩: ٦٣) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ نَزَّلَ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ مَاءً فَأَحْيَا بِهِ الْأَرْضَ مِنْ بَعْدِ مَوْتِهَا لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾ .

**English**:
> (11: 7) ﴿And it is He who created the heavens and the terrestrial earth in six days - and His Throne had been upon water - that He might test you as to which of you is best in deed. But if you say, "Indeed, you are resurrected after death," those who stubbornly reject established reality (Al-Kāfirūn) will surely say, "This is not but obvious magic."﴾ (11: 8) ﴿And if We hold back from them the punishment for a limited time, they will surely say, "What detains it?" Unquestionably, on the Day it comes to them, it will not be averted from them, and they will be enveloped by what they used to ridicule.﴾. (11: 10) ﴿But if We give him a taste of favor after hardship has touched him, he will surely say, "Bad times have left me." Indeed, he is exultant and boastful -﴾. (12: 32) ﴿She said, "That is the one about whom you blamed me. And I certainly sought to seduce him, but he firmly refused; and if he will not do what I order him, he will surely be imprisoned and will be of those debased."﴾. (14: 7) ﴿And remember when your Sovereign Lord proclaimed, 'If you are grateful, I will surely increase you in favor; but if you deny, indeed, My punishment is severe.' "﴾. (17: 62) ﴿Iblees said, "Do You see this one whom You have honored above me? If You delay me until the Day of Resurrection and Accounting (Yawm al-Qiyāmah), I will surely destroy his descendants, except for a few."﴾ (17: 86) ﴿And if We willed, We could surely do away with that which We revealed to you. Then you would not find for yourself concerning it an advocate against Us.﴾ (18: 36) ﴿And I do not think the Hour will occur. And even if I should be brought back to my Sovereign Lord, I will surely find better than this as a return."﴾ (19: 46) ﴿His father said, "Have you no desire for my gods, O Abraham? If you do not desist, I will surely stone you, so avoid me a prolonged time."﴾ (21: 46) ﴿And if as much as a whiff of the punishment of your Sovereign Lord should touch them, they would surely say, "O woe to us! Indeed, we have been wrongdoers."﴾. (24: 53) ﴿And they swear by Allāh their strongest oaths that if you ordered them, they would go forth in Allāh 's cause. Say, "Do not swear. (29: 10) ﴿And of the people are some who say, "We believe in Allāh," but when one of them is harmed for the cause of Allāh, they consider the trial of the people as if it were the punishment of Allāh. But if victory comes from your Sovereign Lord, they say, "Indeed, We were with you." Is not Allāh most knowing of what is within the breasts of all creatures?﴾. (29: 61) ﴿If you asked them, "Who created the heavens and the terrestrial earth and subjected the sun and the moon?" they would surely say, "Allāh." Then how are they deluded?﴾. (29: 63) ﴿And if you asked them, "Who sends down rain from the sky and gives life thereby to the terrestrial earth after its lifelessness?" they would surely say " Allāh." Say, "Praise to Allāh "; but most of them do not reason.﴾.

### Folio 33 (`Page ID: 11928-33` | `165` Words | `11` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> (٣٠: ٥٨) ﴿وَلَئِنْ جِئْتَهُمْ بِآيَةٍ لَيَقُولَنَّ الَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا إِنْ أَنْتُمْ إِلَّا مُبْطِلُونَ﴾ .(٣١: ٢٥) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾ .(٣٣: ٦٠) ﴿لَئِنْ لَمْ يَنْتَهِ الْمُنَافِقُونَ وَالَّذِينَ فِي قُلُوبِهِمْ مَرَضٌ وَالْمُرْجِفُونَ فِي الْمَدِينَةِ لَنُغْرِيَنَّكَ بِهِمْ ثُمَّ لا يُجَاوِرُونَكَ فِيهَا إِلَّا قَلِيلاً﴾ .(٣٥: ٤٢) ﴿وَأَقْسَمُوا بِاللَّهِ جَهْدَ أَيْمَانِهِمْ لَئِنْ جَاءَهُمْ نَذِيرٌ لَيَكُونُنَّ أَهْدَى مِنْ إِحْدَى الْأُمَمِ﴾ .(٣٦: ١٨) ﴿لَئِنْ لَمْ تَنْتَهُوا لَنَرْجُمَنَّكُمْ وَلَيَمَسَّنَّكُمْ مِنَّا عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌ﴾ .(٣٩: ٣٨) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾(٣٩: ٦٥) ﴿لَئِنْ أَشْرَكْتَ لَيَحْبَطَنَّ عَمَلُكَ﴾ .(٤١: ٥٠) ﴿وَلَئِنْ أَذَقْنَاهُ رَحْمَةً مِنَّا مِنْ بَعْدِ ضَرَّاءَ مَسَّتْهُ لَيَقُولَنَّ هَذَا لِي﴾ .(٤٣: ٩) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ لَيَقُولُنَّ خَلَقَهُنَّ الْعَزِيزُ الْعَلِيمُ﴾ .(٤٣: ٨٧) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَهُمْ لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾(٥٩: ١١) ﴿لَئِنْ أُخْرِجْتُمْ لَنَخْرُجَنَّ مَعَكُمْ وَلا نُطِيعُ فِيكُمْ أَحَداً أَبَداً﴾ .(٥٩: ١٢) ﴿وَلَئِنْ نَصَرُوهُمْ لَيُوَلُّنَّ الْأَدْبَارَ ثُمَّ لا يُنْصَرُونَ﴾(٦٣: ٨) ﴿يَقُولُونَ لَئِنْ رَجَعْنَا إِلَى الْمَدِينَةِ لَيُخْرِجَنَّ الْأَعَزُّ مِنْهَا الْأَذَلَّ﴾ .(٩٦: ١٥) ﴿كَلَّا لَئِنْ لَمْ يَنْتَهِ لَنَسْفَعاً بِالنَّاصِيَةِ﴾

**English**:
> (30: 58) ﴿And We have certainly presented to the people in this Qur'an from every kind of example. But, O Messenger, if you should bring them a sign, the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn) will surely say, "You believers are but falsifiers."﴾. (31: 25) ﴿If you asked them, "Who created the heavens and the terrestrial earth and subjected the sun and the moon?" they would surely say, "Allāh." Then how are they deluded?﴾. (33: 60) ﴿If the subversive double-faced deceivers (Al-Munāfiqūn) and those in whose hearts is disease and those who spread rumors in al-Madinah do not cease, We will surely incite you against them; then they will not remain your neighbors therein except for a little.﴾. (35: 42) ﴿And they swore by Allāh their strongest oaths that if a warner came to them, they would be more guided than any one of the previous nations. But when a warner came to them, it did not increase them except in aversion.﴾. (36: 18) ﴿They said, "Indeed, we consider you a bad omen. If you do not desist, we will surely stone you, and there will surely touch you, from us, an painful punishment."﴾. (39: 38) ﴿If you asked them, "Who created the heavens and the terrestrial earth and subjected the sun and the moon?" they would surely say, "Allāh." Then how are they deluded?﴾ (39: 65) ﴿And it was already revealed to you and to those before you that if you should associate anything with Allāh, your work would surely become worthless, and you would surely be among the losers."﴾. (41: 50) ﴿And if We let him taste mercy from Us after an adversity which has touched him, he will surely say, "This is to me, and I do not think the Hour will occur; and even if I should be returned to my Sovereign Lord, indeed, for me there will be with Him the best." But We will surely inform those who rejected established reality about what they did, and We will surely make them taste a massive punishment.﴾. (43: 9) ﴿And if you should ask them, "Who has created the heavens and the terrestrial earth?" they would surely say, "They were created by the Exalted in Might, the Knowing."﴾. (43: 87) ﴿And if you asked them who created them, they would surely say, "Allāh." So how are they deluded?﴾ (59: 11) ﴿Have you not considered those who practice hypocrisy, saying to their brothers who have disbelieved among the People of the Inscribed Scripture, "If you are expelled, we will surely leave with you, and we will not obey, in regard to you, anyone - ever; and if you are fought, we will surely aid you." But Allāh testifies that they are liars.﴾. (59: 12) ﴿If they are expelled, they will not leave with them, and if they are fought, they will not aid them. And even if they should aid them, they will surely turn their backs; then thereafter they will not be aided.﴾ (63: 8) ﴿They say, "If we return to al-Madinah, the more honored for power will surely expel therefrom the more humble." And to Allāh belongs All honor, and to His Messenger, and to those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn), but the subversive double-faced deceivers (Al-Munāfiqūn) do not know.﴾. (96: 15) ﴿Nay! If he does not desist, We will surely drag him by the prefrontal forehead—﴾

- **[S8] AR**: (٤٣: ٩) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَ السَّمَاوَاتِ وَالْأَرْضَ لَيَقُولُنَّ خَلَقَهُنَّ الْعَزِيزُ الْعَلِيمُ﴾ .
  **[S8] EN**: (43: 9) ﴿And if you should ask them, "Who has created the heavens and the terrestrial earth?" they would surely say, "They were created by the Exalted in Might, the Knowing."﴾.
- **[S9] AR**: (٤٣: ٨٧) ﴿وَلَئِنْ سَأَلْتَهُمْ مَنْ خَلَقَهُمْ لَيَقُولُنَّ اللَّهُ﴾ (٥٩: ١١) ﴿لَئِنْ أُخْرِجْتُمْ لَنَخْرُجَنَّ مَعَكُمْ وَلا نُطِيعُ فِيكُمْ أَحَداً أَبَداً﴾ .
  **[S9] EN**: (43: 87) ﴿And if you asked them who created them, they would surely say, "Allāh." So how are they deluded?﴾ (59: 11) ﴿Have you not considered those who practice hypocrisy, saying to their brothers who have disbelieved among the People of the Inscribed Scripture, "If you are expelled, we will surely leave with you, and we will not obey, in regard to you, anyone - ever; and if you are fought, we will surely aid you." But Allāh testifies that they are liars.﴾.
- **[S10] AR**: (٥٩: ١٢) ﴿وَلَئِنْ نَصَرُوهُمْ لَيُوَلُّنَّ الْأَدْبَارَ ثُمَّ لا يُنْصَرُونَ﴾ (٦٣: ٨) ﴿يَقُولُونَ لَئِنْ رَجَعْنَا إِلَى الْمَدِينَةِ لَيُخْرِجَنَّ الْأَعَزُّ مِنْهَا الْأَذَلَّ﴾ .
  **[S10] EN**: (59: 12) ﴿If they are expelled, they will not leave with them, and if they are fought, they will not aid them. And even if they should aid them, they will surely turn their backs; then thereafter they will not be aided.﴾ (63: 8) ﴿They say, "If we return to al-Madinah, the more honored for power will surely expel therefrom the more humble." And to Allāh belongs All honor, and to His Messenger, and to those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn), but the subversive double-faced deceivers (Al-Munāfiqūn) do not know.﴾.

  **[S11] EN**: (96: 15) ﴿Nay! If he does not desist, We will surely drag him by the prefrontal forehead—﴾
